TL;DR: 本研究开发并部署了一个基于嵌入的联邦学习流程，用于从常规全血细胞计数数据预测铁缺乏症，证明了个性化聚合方法FedMAP在处理临床环境中非独立同分布数据时优于标准FedAvg方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近的综述发现，绝大多数已发表的医疗联邦学习研究从未达到实际部署阶段。本研究旨在开发一个实际的联邦学习管道，并在真实临床环境中进行部署验证，以解决医疗联邦学习从研究到实践的转化难题。

Method: 研究开发了基于嵌入的联邦学习管道，使用冻结的血液学基础模型DeepCBC进行站点局部表示提取，将联邦训练限制在紧凑的下游分类器上。该系统部署在阿姆斯特丹大学医学中心(AUMC)和NHS血液与移植中心(NHSBT)两个临床环境中，使用FLA$^3$平台执行运行时治理。研究比较了标准样本大小加权聚合(FedAvg)和个性化聚合方法(FedMAP)的性能。

Result: 两个临床数据集存在结构性非独立同分布(non-IID)特征，异质性源于明显的群体差异。标准FedAvg在两个站点的ROC-AUC均低于本地训练，因为全局更新偏向较大的AUMC分布。FedMAP方法将AUMC的ROC-AUC从0.9470提升至0.9594，将NHSBT的ROC-AUC从0.8558提升至0.8671，实现了最高的宏观ROC-AUC值0.9133和最佳的宏观平衡准确率。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，在客户端样本大小和任务相关性差异显著的临床联邦环境中，个性化聚合方法能够有效提升模型性能。这为医疗联邦学习的实际部署提供了有价值的方法论支持和实践经验。

Abstract: Recent reviews find that the vast majority of published healthcare federated learning (FL) studies never reach real-world deployment. We developed an embedding-based FL pipeline for iron deficiency prediction from routine full blood count (FBC) data and deployed it across real institutional environments at Amsterdam University Medical Centre (AUMC) and NHS Blood and Transplant (NHSBT), two clinical environments that differ markedly in iron deficiency prevalence, ferritin distribution, and subject populations. A frozen domain-specific haematology foundation model, DeepCBC, performs site-local representation extraction, restricting federated training to a compact downstream classifier and substantially reducing recurrent communication relative to full-encoder federation. The two clinical datasets are structurally not independent and identically distributed (non-IID), with heterogeneity arising from distinct population differences rather than sampling artefacts. Runtime governance is enforced by FLA$^3$, a healthcare-oriented FL platform providing study-scoped execution, policy-based authorisation, and signed audit logging. Standard sample-size-weighted aggregation (FedAvg) reduced the area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (ROC-AUC) at both sites relative to local-only training, as the global update was biased towards the larger AUMC distribution. FedMAP, a personalised aggregation method, raised ROC-AUC from 0.9470 to 0.9594 at AUMC and from 0.8558 to 0.8671 at NHSBT relative to local-only training, achieving the highest macro ROC-AUC of 0.9133 and the best macro balanced accuracy overall. These results support personalised aggregation in clinical federations where client sample size and task relevance diverge substantially.

</details>


### [28] [Leveraging Self-Paced Curriculum Learning for Enhanced Modality Balance in Multimodal Conversational Emotion Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21565)
*Phuong-Anh Nguyen,The-Son Le,Duc-Trong Le,Cam-Van Thi Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于自定进度课程学习(SPCL)的即插即用框架，通过双层难度度量器解决多模态对话情感识别中的模态错位和学习不平衡问题，在IEMOCAP和MELD数据集上分别取得了最高+6.6%和+10.4%的F1分数提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对话情感识别中存在模态错位和学习不平衡两大核心挑战，限制了多模态信息的有效利用，现有方法难以充分挖掘不同模态的互补信息。

Method: 提出基于自定进度课程学习(SPCL)的即插即用框架，设计双层难度度量器：(1)话语级别分数建模细粒度模态特定难度；(2)对话级别分数捕获对话结构(情感依赖和模态一致性)；学习调度器根据难度分数动态引导训练过程，从简单到困难样本逐步学习。

Result: 在IEMOCAP数据集上加权F1分数提升约+1.2%至+6.6%，在MELD数据集上提升高达+10.4%；该方法在不同架构和模态设置下均表现出一致的性能改进和良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SPCL作为一种轻量级即插即用模块，能有效缓解模态不平衡问题，提升模型鲁棒性，具有较好的通用性和实用价值，为多模态情感识别提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition in Conversations (MERC) is a crucial task for understanding human interactions, where multimodal approaches integrating language, facial expressions, and vocal tone have achieved significant progress. However, modality misalignment and imbalanced learning remain major challenges, limiting the effective utilization of multimodal information. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play framework based on Self-Paced Curriculum Learning (SPCL) for MERC. We introduce a dual-level Difficulty Measurer that captures both utterance-level and conversation-level challenges. The utterance-level score models fine-grained modality-specific difficulty, while the conversation-level score captures broader dialogue structures, including emotional dependencies and modality coherence. Based on these scores, the Learning Scheduler dynamically guides training from easier to more difficult instances. By integrating SPCL into existing MERC architectures, our method alleviates modality imbalance and improves model robustness. Extensive experiments on the IEMOCAP and MELD datasets demonstrate consistent improvements across different architectures and modality settings. On IEMOCAP, SPCL improves weighted F1-score by approximately +1.2% to +6.6% over baseline models, while on MELD, gains reach up to +10.4%. These results highlight the effectiveness and generalizability of SPCL as a lightweight plug-and-play module for multimodal emotion recognition.

</details>


### [29] [Calibration, Uncertainty Communication, and Deployment Readiness in CKD Risk Prediction: A Framework Evaluation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21566)
*Michael O. Eniolade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在UCI数据集上表现完美的五种CKD预测模型，在MIMIC-IV外部数据集上性能严重退化（AUROC降至0.48-0.58，校准误差激增，覆盖率骤降），且均未通过部署就绪性评估，揭示了内部验证的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有慢性肾脏病（CKD）风险预测的机器学习模型通常仅在内部测试集上报告优异的判别性能（如AUROC），但忽视了模型校准和不确定性量化。这导致临床医生无法判断模型输出的概率是否可靠，影响了模型在真实临床场景中的可信度和实用性。

Method: 在UCI CKD数据集（400名患者）上训练五种分类器（逻辑回归、随机森林、XGBoost、带Platt缩放的SVM、高斯朴素贝叶斯）。从三个维度评估：（1）使用期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数评估校准质量；（2）使用分裂共形预测量化不确定性并 targeting 90%边际覆盖率；（3）使用八项标准的部署就绪性框架评估。采用分布压力测试，将每个模型的最佳校准版本应用于MIMIC-IV演示队列（97名患者，患病率差异显著），评估模型在患病率偏移和特征缺失情况下的表现。同时比较了Platt缩放和等渗回归两种重校准方法的效果。

Result: 所有五种模型在UCI测试集上均达到AUROC 1.00的完美性能，等渗重校准将内部ECE降至0.000-0.022。然而在MIMIC-IV外部数据上：（1）判别能力崩溃：AUROC降至0.48-0.58（接近随机猜测）；（2）校准失效：ECE飙升至0.68-0.76；（3）不确定性量化失败：共形覆盖率从内部测试时的0.80-0.98骤降至0.21-0.25（远低于90%目标）；（4）部署就绪性：所有模型在16分制的部署就绪性检查表中均未超过4分。这一结果清楚表明，近乎完美的内部性能无法转移到外部数据。

Conclusion: 研究结论强调了在临床预测模型部署前，必须在外部数据上评估校准稳定性和共形预测覆盖率。仅依赖内部验证的判别性能指标（如AUROC）具有严重误导性。研究呼吁建立更严格的外部验证标准，特别是在患病率偏移和数据缺失的真实临床环境中，确保模型输出的概率具有实际临床价值。

Abstract: Machine learning models for chronic kidney disease (CKD) risk prediction often post strong discrimination scores on internal test sets. Calibration and uncertainty quantification get far less attention, leaving clinicians without reliable information about whether the probability outputs are accurate.
  We trained five classifiers on the UCI CKD dataset (400 patients, 62.5% CKD prevalence): logistic regression, random forest, XGBoost, SVM with Platt scaling, and Gaussian naive Bayes. We evaluated each across calibration quality, conformal prediction coverage, and an eight-criterion deployment readiness framework. A distributional stress-test applied the best-calibrated variant of each model to the open-access MIMIC-IV demo cohort (97 patients, 23.7% CKD) to assess behaviour under prevalence shift and feature missingness. We measured calibration before and after Platt scaling and isotonic regression using Expected Calibration Error and Brier Score, and quantified uncertainty through split conformal prediction targeting 90% marginal coverage.
  All five models reached AUROC 1.00 on the UCI test set. Isotonic recalibration reduced internal ECE to 0.000-0.022. On MIMIC-IV, AUROC fell to 0.48-0.58, ECE rose to 0.68-0.76, and conformal coverage dropped from 0.80-0.98 to 0.21-0.25 against a 90% target. No model scored above 4 out of 16 on the deployment readiness checklist.
  Near-perfect internal performance did not transfer. Calibration stability and conformal coverage should be evaluated on external data before any clinical prediction model moves toward deployment.

</details>


### [30] [Equilibrium Propagation and Hamiltonian Inference in the Diffusive Fitzhugh-Nagumo Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21568)
*Jack Kendall*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将平衡传播框架扩展到斜梯度系统，建立了深度能量模型与哈密顿神经网络之间的等价性，并以Fitzhugh-Nagumo神经元网络为例展示了该方法的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡传播框架原本局限于特定系统，为了扩展其适用范围并建立不同神经网络范式之间的理论联系，作者将平衡传播框架扩展到更一般的斜梯度系统，特别是生物可解释的Fitzhugh-Nagumo神经元网络。

Method: 利用Fitzhugh-Nagumo模型的稳态解可由自伴算子描述这一性质，将平衡传播方法应用于信用分配；对于具有深度残差网络拓扑的Fitzhugh-Nagumo网络，利用其稳态解的空间哈密顿结构，应用哈密顿回声反向传播方法。

Result: 证明了Fitzhugh-Nagumo网络稳态解可用自伴算子描述，从而平衡传播方法适用；发现具有深度残差网络拓扑的Fitzhugh-Nagumo网络稳态解具有空间哈密顿结构，可应用哈密顿回声反向传播；推导了深度Fitzhugh-Nagumo网络和深度能量模型稳态解的显式逐层哈密顿递推关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了深度能量模型与哈密顿神经网络之间的理论等价性，扩展了平衡传播框架的适用范围至斜梯度系统，为生物可解释的神经网络学习算法提供了新的理论基础和计算框架。

Abstract: In this work, we extend the Equilibrium Propagation framework to skew-gradient systems and show an equivalence between deep Energy-Based Models and Hamiltonian neural networks. We focus on networks of diffusively coupled Fitzhugh-Nagumo neurons as a prototypical example. We show that since stationary solutions of the Fitzhugh-Nagumo model are described by self-adjoint operators, the methods of equilibrium propagation for performing credit assignment can be applied. Furthermore, for Fitzhugh-Nagumo networks with the topology of a deep residual network, we show that the steady state solutions admit a (spatial) Hamiltonian, and thus the methods of Hamiltonian Echo Backpropagation can be applied. We end by deriving an explicit layer-wise Hamiltonian recurrence relation governing inference for stationary solutions of both deep Fitzhugh-Nagumo networks and deep Energy-Based Models.

</details>


### [31] [ConTact: Contact-First Antibody CDR Design via Explicit Interface Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21600)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Spencer VonBank,Nadeem Taj,Sujin Lee,Naila Jan,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConTact提出接触-行动架构，通过显式分解CDR设计问题（先预测接触位置，再设计氨基酸序列），在CHIMERA-Bench上实现了最佳结构质量和表位感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算抗体CDR设计方法将两个本质不同的子问题混为一谈：识别哪些CDR位置会与抗原接触，以及在这些位置选择氨基酸。这种混淆迫使模型通过均匀消息传递隐式学习接触推理，稀释了抗原信号。

Method: 提出ConTact架构，将CDR设计显式分解为三个级联阶段：学习表面互补性指纹、预测CDR-抗原接触、将接触门控的抗原特征注入序列头。采用距离偏置交叉注意力模块编码几何先验，以及接触加权交叉熵损失将梯度信号集中在结合关键位置。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上，ConTact实现了最佳结构质量（RMSD相比次优基线提升7%）、最佳表位感知能力（F1分数相比GNN基线提升10%）以及具有竞争力的序列恢复率（AAR 0.38）。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦接触预测和序列设计两个子问题，ConTact架构有效提升了抗体CDR设计的性能，证明了分解策略在计算抗体设计中的有效性。

Abstract: Computational antibody CDR design methods condition on antigen structure to generate binding loops, yet existing architectures conflate two fundamentally distinct sub-problems: identifying which CDR positions will contact the antigen, and selecting amino acids at those positions. This conflation forces models to learn contact reasoning implicitly through uniform message passing, diluting antigen signal across all positions equally. We introduce ConTact, a contact-then-act architecture that explicitly decomposes CDR design into three cascaded stages: learning surface complementarity fingerprints, predicting CDR-antigen contacts, and injecting contact-gated antigen features into the sequence head. A distance-biased cross-attention module encodes geometric priors favoring spatial neighbors, while a contact-weighted cross-entropy loss concentrates gradient signal on binding-critical positions. On CHIMERA-Bench dataset, ConTact achieves the best structural quality (7% RMSD improvement over the next-best baseline), best epitope awareness (10% F1 score over GNN baselines), and competitive sequence recovery (AAR 0.38) among several CDR-H3 design baselines.

</details>


### [32] [When Are Teacher Tokens Reliable? Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation for Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21606)
*Xiaogeng Liu,Xinyan Wang,Yingzi Ma,Yechao Zhang,Chaowei Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了位置加权在线自蒸馏方法(PW-OPSD)，通过发现推理蒸馏中教师token可靠性与序列位置强相关，解决了传统方法对所有生成token同等对待的问题，在AIME数学推理任务上取得了1.0-1.1分的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略自蒸馏(OPSD)的标准目标对所有生成的token赋予相同权重，隐含假设特权教师目标在学生访问的每个前缀处都具有相同的可靠性。现有的基于熵的OPD方法通过教师熵来调节token级别的监督，但推理过程中高教师熵的可靠性含义模糊：可能反映不可行的确定性或良性的解法多样性。本文旨在识别并利用教师token可靠性的轨迹结构。

Method: 首先引入分支可行性诊断，记录特权答案教师提示的下一个token备选项，强制每个备选项跟随学生提示及其在线策略主干前缀，测试结果学生模板续写是否能恢复正确答案。发现序列内的定向位置分数是教师token可靠性的最强预测因子(AUROC 0.83)，远超局部不确定性分数(最高0.57)。基于此轨迹级结构，提出位置加权在线策略自蒸馏(PW-OPSD)，在保持相同的学生rollout、特权教师传递和裁剪前向KL目标的同时，应用递增的位置权重。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B模型上，PW-OPSD在AIME 2024和AIME 2025数据集上Avg@12分别提升+1.0和+1.1分。在更大规模的不同模型系列(DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B和Olmo-3-7B-Think)上的泛化评估也显示出一致的Avg@12提升。位置分数的预测能力(AUROC 0.83)显著优于局部不确定性分数(最高0.57)。

Conclusion: 推理蒸馏中的教师token可靠性具有轨迹结构特征，可以通过序列位置进行有效预测和利用，无需额外的教师计算。PW-OPSD方法通过利用这一结构化特征，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了推理蒸馏的性能，证明了位置加权策略的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) trains a student on its own rollouts using a privileged teacher, but its standard objective weights all generated tokens equally, implicitly treating the privileged teacher target as equally reliable at every student-visited prefix. Existing entropy-based OPD methods relax this uniformity by modulating token-level supervision with teacher entropy, but high teacher entropy in reasoning has an ambiguous reliability meaning: it can reflect either non-viable uncertainty or benign solution diversity. To identify this phenomenon, we introduce a branch-viability diagnostic. Specifically, we record next-token alternatives from the privileged-answer teacher prompt, force each alternative after the student prompt plus its on-policy spine prefix, and test whether the resulting student-template continuation recovers the correct answer. On Qwen3-4B, we find that an oriented within-sequence position score is the strongest tested predictor of teacher-token reliability, reaching an area-under-ROC-curve (AUROC) of 0.83; local uncertainty scores are at most 0.57. Motivated by this trajectory-level structure, we propose Position-Weighted On-Policy Self-Distillation (PW-OPSD), which applies an increasing position weight while keeping the same student rollout, privileged teacher pass, and clipped forward-KL target as OPSD. In our comprehensive evaluations with different random seeds, the diagnostic-derived PW-OPSD improves AIME 2024 and AIME 2025 Avg@12 by +1.0 and +1.1 points, and a generalization evaluation on two larger-scale models from different families, DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Llama-8B and Olmo-3-7B-Think, also demonstrates consistent aggregate Avg@12 improvements. These results show that teacher-token reliability in reasoning distillation is trajectory-structured and can be utilized without additional teacher computation.

</details>


### [33] [AgForce Enables Antigen-conditioned Generative Antibody Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21610)
*Mansoor Ahmed,Murray Patterson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 现有抗体设计方法忽视抗原输入，作者提出AgForce架构通过改进编解码器设计解决该问题，在CHIMERA-Bench上实现最优结合质量和序列恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统评估发现现有抗体设计方法存在三个失效模式：(1)抗原盲性——模型从抗体框架上下文而非抗原信息预测，无视目标产生近乎相同的CDR；(2)词汇崩塌——每位点预测氨基酸降至3-5个，远低于天然序列分布；(3)标准交叉熵训练使模型收敛到位置边缘分布，理论上无法产生抗原特异性预测。

Method: 提出AgForce编码器-解码器架构：编码器采用图神经网络(GNN)，应用框架丢弃、门控瓶颈和双曲交叉注意力阻断抗体捷径路径；解码器使用混合密度网络(MDN)序列头，结合Potts类成对耦合和退火多选择学习(aMCL)，用多分量分布替代交叉熵目标，并引入抗原循环一致性头强制预测分布编码抗原身份。

Result: 在CHIMERA-Bench数据集上同时实现最佳结合质量和序列恢复，氨基酸恢复率比最强序列基线提升8%，在所有界面指标上超越基线，有效词汇量较GNN方法提升近一倍。

Conclusion: AgForce成功解决了现有方法的抗原盲性问题，实现了真正的抗原条件化抗体设计，为序列-结构协同设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Antibody design methods condition on antigen structure to generate complementarity-determining regions (CDR), yet a systematic evaluation of baseline methods reveals that they largely ignore the antigen input. We identify three failure modes that explain this behavior. Antigen blindness arises because models derive predictions from antibody framework context rather than antigen information, producing nearly identical CDRs regardless of the target. Vocabulary collapse reduces predicted amino acids to three to five per position, far below the ground truth distribution in native sequences. Moreover, any model trained with standard per-position cross-entropy converges to the positional marginal distribution, making it provably unable to produce antigen-specific sequence predictions. We propose a novel encoder-decoder architecture called AgForce, that uses a graph neural network (GNN) as the encoder and specialized decoders for sequence-structure co-design. Specifically, we apply framework dropout, gated bottlenecks, and hyperbolic cross attention that prevent the antibody shortcut path. In the decoder, a Mixture Density Network (MDN) sequence head with Potts-like pairwise coupling and annealed Multiple Choice Learning (aMCL) replaces the cross-entropy objective with a multi-component distribution whose optimal solution differs from the positional marginal. An antigen cycle consistency head routes gradients through the sequence decoder, forcing predicted distributions to encode antigen identity. AgForce achieves the best binding quality and sequence recovery simultaneously on the CHIMERA-Bench dataset, improving amino acid recovery by 8% over the strongest sequence baseline while surpassing the baselines across all interface metrics, and nearly doubling the effective vocabulary of GNN methods. The source code is available at: https://github.com/mansoor181/ag-force.git

</details>


### [34] [$\textit{BlockFormer}$ : Transformer-based inference from interaction maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21617)
*Eloïse Touron,Pedro L. C. Rodrigues,Julyan Arbel,Nelle Varoquaux,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，用于解决交互图谱（如Hi-C数据）中的逆问题，成功实现了跨物种的着丝粒定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互图谱（如Hi-C技术产生的基因组染色体构象捕获数据）的推断问题可形式化为通用逆问题，即根据实体间成对交互的区块映射推断参数。现有方法需要处理现实数据中实体数量和大小的可变性，并利用图谱间的共享结构（如局部模式的全局对齐）。

Method: 提出基于Transformer架构的数据驱动方法，能够处理实体数量和大小的可变性；设计了定制模拟器生成大量计算成本低廉的合成数据用于训练。该方法充分利用图谱间的共享结构特性。

Result: 在着丝粒定位问题上，该方法能够在广泛的物种范围内准确恢复着丝粒的基因组位置，适用于不同基因组大小的物种。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了交互图谱分析中的逆问题，通过Transformer架构和合成数据训练策略，实现了跨物种的稳健性能，为基因组结构分析提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: Inference from interaction maps, such as centromere identification from genome-wide chromosome conformation capture techniques -- notably Hi-C -- can be formulated as a generic inverse problem: infer a set of parameters given a map summarizing pairwise interactions between entities through blocks of variable numbers and sizes. In this work, we introduce a data-driven approach that leverages shared structure between these maps, such as global alignment between localized patterns, while handling the variability in number and size of entities arising in real-world data. Our approach relies on a transformer architecture capable of handling such variability and a custom simulator to generate abundant, yet computationally cheap synthetic data for training. Applied to the problem of centromere localization, the method accurately recovers their genomic positions across a wide range of species of various genome sizes.

</details>


### [35] [Alike Parts: A Feature-Informed Approach to Local and Global Prototype Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21646)
*Jacek Karolczak,Jerzy Stefanowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种在两个层级整合特征重要性的原型解释框架：局部解释识别“相似部分”，全局原型选择促进特征归因多样性，在保持模型保真度的同时提升了可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于原型的解释方法虽然直观，但缺乏特征层面的粒度，无法精细地解释模型决策依据。

Method: 提出一个双层整合特征重要性的框架：1）局部解释层面，提出“相似部分”方法，利用特征重要性分数高亮分类实例与其最近原型之间最相关的共享特征子集；2）全局层面，在原型选择目标函数中增加特征重要性项，主动促进所选原型在特征归因上的多样性。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的实验表明，增强的选择过程能够保持甚至在某些情况下提高代理模型的预测保真度，证明特征多样性不会损害模型保真度。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将特征粒度整合到原型解释中，在提升可解释性的同时未牺牲模型性能，为黑盒分类器提供了一种有效的解释方法。

Abstract: Prototype-based explanations offer an intuitive, example-based approach to support the interpretability of machine learning black box classifiers but often lack feature-level granularity. We introduce a framework that integrates feature importance at two levels to address this gap. First, for local explanations, we propose \textit{alike parts}: a method that uses feature importance scores to highlight the most relevant, shared feature subsets between a classified instance and its nearest prototype, guiding user attention. Second, we augment the global prototype selection objective function with a feature importance term to actively promote diversity in the feature attributions of the selected prototypes. Experiments on six benchmark datasets show that this augmented selection process maintains or, in some cases, increases the prediction fidelity of the surrogate model, suggesting that feature diversity does not compromise model fidelity.

</details>


### [36] [Dropout Universality: Scaling Laws and Optimal Scheduling at the Edge-of-Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21648)
*Lucas Fernandez Sarmiento*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了Dropout的均值场理论，揭示其在临界信号传播中的微扰效应；发现平滑激活与类ReLU激活分属不同普适类，并通过理论推导提出了显著降低测试损失的前置式Dropout调度策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dropout作为一种广泛使用的正则化技术，其理论机制尚不完全清晰。本文旨在从临界信号传播与边缘混沌理论出发，建立Dropout的理论框架，理解其对深度网络信息传播的影响，并指导更有效的Dropout策略设计。

Method: 将Dropout建模为边缘混沌处临界信号传播的微扰，推导临界与交叉尺度律；利用关联映射的解析结构分析，区分平滑激活函数（可泰勒展开）与类ReLU激活函数（具有分支点非解析性）的普适类特征；通过双参数标度塌缩验证理论预测，并推导固定预算下的饱和Dropout分布。

Result: 发现Dropout移动完美对齐不动点，使临界初始化下的深度尺度有限；建立了关联衰减的临界指数，证明平滑与kinked激活函数构成不同普适类；理论预测的前置式Dropout调度（由rank-flow决胜规则选择）在MLP和Vision Transformer上显著降低held-out测试损失（无额外计算成本），准确率提升作为次要效应。

Conclusion: 本文从统计物理视角建立了Dropout的理论框架，揭示了不同激活函数的普适类差异，并提出了实用的前置式Dropout调度策略，为理解与优化正则化技术提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We develop a mean-field theory of dropout as a perturbation of critical signal propagation at the edge of chaos. Dropout shifts the perfect-alignment fixed point, making the depth scale for information propagation finite even at critical initialization. We derive critical and crossover scaling laws for correlation decay and establish that smooth activations and kinked, ReLU-like activations constitute distinct universality classes, with different critical exponents and a universal two-parameter scaling collapse in detuning and dropout strength. The distinction traces to the analytic structure of the correlation map: smooth activations admit a Taylor expansion near perfect alignment, while kinked activations develop a branch point with universal non-analyticity. As a corollary, the framework yields saturated dropout profiles under fixed budget; a rank-flow tie-breaker then selects front-loaded schedules, substantially reducing held-out test loss at no extra computational cost, with accuracy gains as a consistent secondary effect. We test the predictions in MLPs and Vision Transformers and discuss CNN/ResNet extensions.

</details>


### [37] [EntmaxKV: Support-Aware Decoding for Entmax Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21649)
*Gonçalo Duarte,Miguel Couceiro,Marcos V. Treviso*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EntmaxKV，一种基于α-entmax的稀疏解码框架，通过在加载KV页之前利用稀疏性，显著减少了长上下文解码中的KV缓存内存流量，相比全注意力基线实现了最高5.43倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受到KV缓存内存流量的严重限制，因为每个生成的token都需要关注一个随上下文长度线性增长的缓存。现有的稀疏解码方法针对softmax注意力设计，但其密集尾部特性使得任何截断都会丢弃非零概率质量。而α-entmax能产生精确的零值，将稀疏解码从密集尾部近似转变为支持集恢复问题。

Method: 提出了EntmaxKV框架，结合了三个核心组件：(1)查询感知的页面评分，(2)支持集感知的候选选择，(3)稀疏entmax注意力。引入了高斯感知的entmax选择器，通过轻量级页面统计信息估计entmax阈值，自适应地调整选择预算。通过丢弃概率质量δ来分析截断误差，证明了输出误差由δ控制且在entmax支持集恢复时消失。

Result: 在相同KV预算下，EntmaxKV相比基于softmax的稀疏解码丢弃更少的概率质量，保留更多的支持集token，实现更低的输出误差。在长上下文和语言建模基准测试中，使用小部分KV缓存即可接近全缓存entmax的性能，在1M上下文长度下相比softmax基线实现3.36倍加速，相比entmax基线实现5.43倍加速。

Conclusion: EntmaxKV成功地将稀疏解码从密集尾部近似转变为精确的支持集恢复，充分利用了entmax的稀疏特性，在保持模型性能的同时显著降低了内存和计算开销，为长上下文解码提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is increasingly limited by KV-cache memory traffic since each generated token attends over a cache whose size grows linearly with context length. Existing sparse decoding methods reduce this cost by selecting subsets of tokens or pages, but are designed for softmax attention, whose dense tails make any truncation discard nonzero probability mass. In contrast, $α$-entmax produces exact zeros, turning sparse decoding from dense-tail approximation into support recovery: if the selected candidates contain the entmax support, sparse decoding remains exact. While recent entmax kernels enable efficient training, they do not address the autoregressive decoding bottleneck, where dense inference still streams the full KV cache before sparsity is known. In this work, we introduce EntmaxKV, an entmax-native sparse decoding framework that exploits sparsity before KV pages are loaded. EntmaxKV combines query-aware page scoring, support-aware candidate selection, and sparse entmax attention. We analyze truncation error through the dropped probability mass $δ$, showing that output error is controlled by $δ$ and vanishes when the entmax support is recovered. We further introduce a Gaussian-aware entmax selector that estimates the entmax threshold from lightweight page statistics, adapting the selected budget to the score distribution. Empirically, EntmaxKV drops less probability mass, retains more support tokens, and achieves lower output error than softmax-based sparse decoding at matched KV budgets. On long-context and language modeling benchmarks, it closely matches full-cache entmax while using a small fraction of the KV cache, achieving up to $3.36\times$ (softmax) and $5.43\times$ (entmax) speedup over full attention baselines at 1M context length. Code available at: https://github.com/deep-spin/entmaxkv.

</details>


### [38] [Amplifying, Not Learning: Fine-Tuned AI Text Detectors Amplify a Pretrained Direction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21653)
*Alexander Smirnov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AI文本检测器并非构建人机边界，而是放大了预训练典型性轴。原始编码器投影即可达到微调性能的86-106%，在非母语写作上该轴发生反转（AUROC 0.06-0.20），验证了典型性假说的独特预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究AI文本检测器的内在机制：它们是否真正构建了人机文本的判别边界，还是仅仅放大了预训练模型中已存在的某种典型性特征。这一问题的澄清对理解检测器的工作原理、局限性及公平性具有重要意义。

Method: 1. 在原始编码器（无任务监督）上，将表示投影到centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3)方向进行检测
2. 使用24样本冻结探针匹配全微调性能
3. 提出闭式Jacobian预测器参数化轴操控干预
4. 采用三种操作上不同的探针（文本表面caps_rate残差化、几何符号ε消融、闭式文本对预测器）验证观察者不变性
5. 在匹配TPR=0.90条件下评估已发表的干预方法

Result: 1. 原始投影在三个架构上达到AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834（微调上限的86-106%）；RoBERTa-base上原始投影超越微调
2. 该轴在非母语ESL写作上反转（AUROC 0.06-0.20）——典型性解读的独特可证伪预测
3. 24样本冻结探针匹配全微调（0.900 vs 0.895）
4. 闭式Jacobian预测器实现R²=1.000，将ELECTRA-CE部署TPR从0.000提升至0.904（FPR=1%），迁移至三个第三方检测器
5. 三种不同探针在三架构上余弦相似度达0.74/0.81/1.00，确认观察者不变性
6. 已发表干预方法在27个单元格中校准等价（|ΔAUROC|≤0.0081），ELECTRA上≥97%的LoRA→全微调偏差差距是校准偏移而非学习表征

Conclusion: AI文本检测器放大的是预训练典型性轴而非构建真正的人机边界。这一机制解释了检测器在非母语写作上的失败（轴反转），并提示检测偏见可能源于模型内部的固有特征而非任务特定学习。研究为理解检测器局限性和设计更公平的检测系统提供了理论框架。

Abstract: AI text detectors amplify a pretrained typicality axis; they do not construct an AI-vs-human boundary. On raw encoders before any task supervision, projecting onto centroid(AI)-centroid(HC3) achieves NYT-vs-HC3 AUROC 0.806/0.944/0.834 across three architectures (86-106% of the fine-tuned discrimination ceiling: on RoBERTa-base, raw projection exceeds fine-tuning); on RoBERTa-base, full fine-tuning reduces discrimination below raw on both fluent-formal populations tested. The same axis inverts on non-native ESL writing (AUROC 0.06-0.20) -- a falsifiable prediction unique to the typicality reading. A 24-example frozen probe matches full fine-tuning (0.900 vs 0.895). A closed-form Jacobian predictor parameterises axis-manipulating interventions with R^2 = 1.000 universal, lifts ELECTRA-CE deployment TPR from 0.000 to 0.904 at FPR = 1%, and transfers to three independently-trained third-party RoBERTa detectors at 16/16 oracle-equivalence (57% NYT-FPR reduction on the OpenAI detector). Scope: encoder family; mechanism magnitude HC3-anchored; population-level shared axis with per-text mechanisms varying across architectures. Three operationally distinct probes -- text-surface caps_rate residualisation, geometric signed-epsilon ablation, closed-form text-pair predictor -- agree at cos 0.74/0.81/1.00 across three architectures, confirming observer-invariance. Under matched-TPR-0.90 evaluation, the published intervention zoo (CC, dealign-f2c) is calibration-equivalent across 27 cells (|Delta AUROC| <= 0.0081), and >= 97% of the LoRA->full-FT bias gap on ELECTRA is calibration shift, not learned representation -- the central claim's prediction confirmed.

</details>

Abstract: Reinforcement learning substantially improves pretrained language models, but it remains understudied why critic-free methods such as PPO and GRPO work as well as they do, and when they should provide the largest gains. First, under a differentiable rollout and additive-noise parameterization, we show that the actor update is value-gradient-like in expectation: the backward pass propagates costates whose conditional expectation equals the value gradient. Second, for discrete transformer policies, we show that autodifferentiation through attention produces empirical costates that approximate this value signal, with an error controlled by the sampling gap and policy entropy. These results motivate a decomposition of RL impact into value gradient signal and reachable reward headroom, yielding a criterion for when RL should be most effective along a pretraining trajectory.

Abstract: Adapting pretrained diffusion models to downstream objectives such as inverse problems often requires expensive test-time guidance or optimization. Our approach formulates test-time adaptation as a hierarchical variational model, where control is amortized into a lightweight yet expressive stochastic policy. This formulation naturally supports few-step diffusion sampling: large step sizes enable fast inference, while the learned policy maintains sample quality by providing structured per-step control. The resulting fully amortized sampler achieves a strong quality--speed tradeoff, matching or exceeding recent test-time scaling baselines while requiring significantly less compute. For example, on 4x super-resolution, our method achieves better perceptual quality with more than 5x faster inference compared to the best-performing baseline. We further extend our approach to a semi-amortized regime that combines cheap amortized proposals with limited test-time optimization, achieving state-of-the-art perceptual quality across several challenging inverse problems.

Result: 证明表征差距由单一参数——任务的内在维度控制，该参数易于解释、估计效率高，并能与常见神经网络架构的等变性建立联系。渐近动力学可扩展到更广泛的任务和训练算法。在合成数据集和真实数据集上的实验验证了渐近定律和内在维度估计的准确性。

Conclusion: 提出的表征差距及其内在维度参数为神经网络的泛化误差表征提供了理论基础和实用工具，该方法在多种数据集上表现良好，结果与相关文献一致，为神经网络设计选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Characterizing precisely the asymptotic generalization error of neural networks using parameters that can be estimated efficiently is a crucial problem in machine learning, which relies heavily on heuristics and practitioners' intuition to make key design choices. In order to mitigate this issue, we introduce the Representation Gap, a metric closely related to the generalization error, but admitting better-behaved asymptotic dynamics. Focusing on equivariant diffusion models and leveraging results from optimal quantization and point-process theory, we derive a precise asymptotic equivalent of the Representation Gap and show that it is governed by a single parameter, the \textit{intrinsic dimension} of the task, which is easy to interpret, efficient to estimate, and can be linked to the equivariances of common neural network architectures. We show that this asymptotic dynamic also extends to a broader range of tasks and training algorithms. Finally, we demonstrate empirically that our asymptotic law and intrinsic dimension estimation are accurate on a wide range of synthetic datasets, where these quantities are known, as well as on more realistic datasets, where we obtain results consistent with the related literature.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨tokenizer知识蒸馏方法在处理完整输出分布时存在两个关键缺陷：(1)罕见token失效——关键token落入未匹配子集被抑制，导致性能急剧下降（如GSM8k从12.89降至2.56）；(2)过度保守匹配——严格的1对1匹配排除了跨表面形式的近似等价token。这两个问题需要不同的解决方案：当关键token错位时消除划分，当对齐可靠时细化划分。

Method: 提出X-Token方法，包含两个互补的损失函数：(1) P-KL通过稀疏投影矩阵W消除划分，将学生分布与教师分布对齐，W由tokenizer级别的字符串规则初始化，解决罕见token失效问题；(2) H-KL保留混合形式但放宽匹配，将每个学生token与其在W下的顶级教师映射对齐。两个目标共享W并自然扩展到多教师场景。

Result: 在Llama-3.2-1B上的实验表明：使用Qwen3-4B教师时，X-Token比当前最优方法GOLD平均提升+3.82分；使用Phi-4-Mini教师时提升+0.5分；双教师设置（Phi-4-mini + Llama-3B）比单教师蒸馏进一步提升+1.3分。

Conclusion: X-Token通过针对不同失效模式设计互补的损失函数，有效解决了跨tokenizer知识蒸馏中的关键问题，显著提升了蒸馏性能，并验证了多教师蒸馏的可行性。

Abstract: Cross-tokenizer knowledge distillation allows a student model to learn from teachers with incompatible vocabularies. Prior work operates on hidden states or logits; the latter is preferred as a drop-in replacement requiring no auxiliary components. Logit-based methods either use only the correct-token probability, missing the full 'dark knowledge' in the teacher's distribution, or operate on the full output distribution, relying on strict token partitioning and/or unprincipled heuristic ranking. We identify two key shortcomings of full-distribution, logit-based methods: (i) an uncommon-token failure, where critical tokens fall into the unmatched subset (e.g., Llama's 1100 multi-digit numerals under digit-splitting Qwen supervision) and are suppressed during training, reducing GSM8k from 12.89 to 2.56 compared to same-tokenizer KD from a weaker teacher; and (ii) over-conservative matching, where strict 1-to-1 matching excludes near-equivalent tokens across surface forms. These failures require distinct remedies: eliminating the partition when critical tokens are misaligned, and refining it when alignment is reliable. We propose X-Token, an approach with two complementary loss formulations targeting these issues. P-KL removes partitioning and aligns the student's distribution with the teacher's via a sparse projection matrix W (initialized from tokenizer-level string rules) to address the uncommon-token failure. H-KL retains the hybrid form while relaxing matching to align each student token with its top-ranked teacher mapping under W. Both objectives share W and extend naturally to multiple teachers. Empirically, on Llama-3.2-1B, X-Token outperforms the current state of the art GOLD by +3.82 average points with a Qwen3-4B teacher and by +0.5 with a Phi-4-Mini teacher. Further, a two-teacher setup (Phi-4-mini + Llama-3B) improves over single-teacher distillation by +1.3 points.

</details>

Method: 提出Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP)参数化方法及其递归变体RTBP，能够构造具有$(n-1)^2$个自由度的精确双随机混合矩阵。该方法避免了迭代归一化过程和组合爆炸问题，同时保持了Birkhoff多胞形的完整表达能力。

Result: 在语言模型预训练任务的实验中，该方法展现出具有竞争力的性能表现，同时在稳定性和可扩展性方面有所提升。

Conclusion: TBP/RTBP参数化成功地在精确双随机约束、完整表达能力和计算效率之间取得了平衡，为残差网络的多流混合机制提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Hyper-Connections (HC) improve residual networks by introducing learnable mixing across multiple residual streams, but unconstrained mixing leads to training instability. Manifold-Constrained Hyper-Connections (mHC) address this by enforcing approximate double stochasticity via Sinkhorn normalization, while mHC-lite ensures exact constraints through convex combinations of permutation matrices at the cost of factorial complexity. KromHC reduces this cost using Kronecker-product parameterizations, but restricts the mixing matrices to a structured submanifold of the Birkhoff polytope .
  We propose Transportation Birkhoff Polytope (TBP) parameterizations and their Recursive variants (RTBP), which construct exactly doubly stochastic mixing matrices with $(n-1)^2$ degrees of freedom. Our approach avoids iterative normalization and combinatorial explosion while preserving full expressivity of the Birkhoff polytope. Empirical results on language model pre-training' demonstrate competitive performance with improved stability and scalability.

</details>

*Barbara Tarantino,Gennaro Auricchio,Paolo Giudici*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了I-SAFE框架，一种用于科学AI模型的事后分布审计方法，通过Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM）等三种互补指标，在结构引导的输入扰动下评估模型输出的分布相干性，揭示传统准确率评估无法发现的模型行为差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在科学预测任务中被广泛使用，但高基准性能可能被误解为具有科学意义的行为。模型可能利用捷径特征、数据集特定规律或分布偏差来获得良好的预测性能，而这些特征与领域相关结构并不一致。这种解释的脆弱性促使作者开发一种新的审计框架。

Method: 提出I-SAFE框架，这是一种事后分布审计框架。给定训练好的黑盒预测器和编码领域知识的外部结构先验，I-SAFE在结构引导的输入扰动下评估原始模型输出。框架包含三种互补度量：（1）分位数度量（QBM）用于位置级相干性；（2）Wasserstein相干性度量（WCM）用于序数相干性；（3）平移不变WCM变体用于形状相干性。该方法在药物-靶点相互作用（DTI）预测任务上进行了验证，使用Davis激酶基准、KLIFS结合口袋注释，以及DeepConvDTI、DeepDTA和TAPB三个序列模型。

Result: 尽管三个DTI模型在预测性能上处于可比范围，I-SAFE揭示了它们具有显著不同的分布响应特征，这一区别在基于准确率的评估中是不可见的。这表明即使预测性能相似的模型，其对结构扰动的响应模式可能存在本质差异。

Conclusion: I-SAFE框架成功识别了传统评估方法无法发现的模型行为差异，证明了分布审计对于科学AI模型的重要性。该框架具有模型无关性，可应用于任何输入可进行结构分解且存在外部先验的领域。

Abstract: Deep learning models are increasingly used in scientific prediction tasks where strong benchmark performance is often interpreted as evidence of scientifically meaningful behavior. This interpretation is fragile, as models may exploit shortcut features, dataset-specific regularities, or distributional biases that are predictive on held-out data but not aligned with domain-relevant structure. To address this limitation, we introduce the \textsc{I-SAFE} (Interventional Secure, Accurate, Fair and Explainable) framework, a post-hoc distributional auditing framework for scientific AI models centered on the Wasserstein Coherence Metric (WCM). Given a trained black-box predictor and an external structural prior encoding domain knowledge about task-relevant input structure, \textsc{I-SAFE} evaluates raw model outputs under structurally guided perturbations of the input. The proposed audit measures output-distribution coherence through three complementary metrics: a Quantile-Based Metric (QBM) for location-level coherence, the WCM for ordinal coherence, and a translation-invariant WCM variant for shape coherence. We instantiate \textsc{I-SAFE} on drug--target interaction (DTI) prediction using the Davis kinase benchmark, KLIFS (Kinase--Ligand Interaction Fingerprints and Structures) binding-pocket annotations, and three sequence-based DTI models: DeepConvDTI, DeepDTA, and TAPB. Although the models operate in a comparable predictive regime, \textsc{I-SAFE} reveals substantially different distributional response profiles, a distinction invisible to accuracy-based evaluation. The framework is model-agnostic and applicable to any domain where inputs admit a structured decomposition and an external prior is available.

</details>

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PFNs在表格分类任务中表现优异，但在类别不平衡情况下性能下降，导致稀有类别表现不佳。现有的缓解类别不平衡的技术中，由于PFNs的上下文学习（ICL）特性，基于损失的策略无法应用，其他技术的效果也未经证实。

Method: 作者将几种经典的解决类别不平衡的技术适配到PFNs中，并在PFN分类任务上分析比较了这些技术的性能表现。

Result: 实验结果表明，由于PFNs的校准特性，阈值调整方法表现尤为出色；由于PFNs在有限数据上的优异性能，下采样方法也能达到可比的效果，同时还具有降低推理计算成本的额外优势。

Conclusion: 针对PFN分类器中的类别不平衡问题，阈值调整和下采样是两种有效且实用的解决方案，其中下采样方法在保持性能的同时还能提升计算效率。

Abstract: Prior-data fitted networks (PFNs) have achieved exceptional performance on tabular classification tasks. However, like other classifiers, their performance can suffer under the effect of class imbalance, resulting in poor performance for rare classes. Several techniques exist which attempt to mitigate the deleterious effect of class imbalance on classification performance, but the in-context learning (ICL) dynamic of PFNs means that loss-based strategies are impossible, and other techniques are unproven. We have adapted several classical techniques addressing class imbalance and analyzed their performance on PFN classification. We observe that thresholding performs exceptionally well because of the calibration characteristics of PFNs, and downsampling performs comparably because of PFNs exceptional limited-data performance, with the additional benefit of reduced computation cost for inference.

</details>

Conclusion: 成功开发了基于常规非增强CTCS扫描预测心肌缺血的机器学习方法。钙化组学特征在传统危险因素和Agatston评分基础上提供了增量预测价值，可能支持更易及的心血管风险分层。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is widely recognized as an effective tool for cardiovascular risk stratification. This study aimed to develop a novel machine learning framework for predicting myocardial ischemia from routine non-contrast CTCS scans using quantitative coronary calcium assessment. This study analyzed 1,375 patients who underwent both non-contrast CTCS and regadenoson stress cardiac positron emission tomography myocardial perfusion imaging within one year at University Hospitals Cleveland Medical Center. A total of 74 variables, including clinical variables, Agatston score, and calcium-omics features, were evaluated. Relevant features were identified using XGBoost with Shapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP). Predictive models were trained and evaluated using 5-fold cross-validation. Among 987 patients, 89 (9%) were positive for myocardial ischemia. The final model incorporated the Agatston score, eight calcium-omics features, and age. The proposed model achieved a precision of 98.9+/-3.0%, sensitivity of 79.2+/-8.4, and F1 score of 87.7+/-5.3%. The addition of calcium-omics features significantly improved predictive performance compared with models using clinical variables alone or clinical variables with the Agatston score (p<0.05). Interestingly, the number of calcified arteries, despite being the lowest-ranked feature based on SHAP analysis, showed the strongest association with myocardial ischemia in logistic regression analysis (odds ratio: 3.63, 95% confidence interval: 2.80-4.77, p<0.00001). We developed a machine learning approach for predicting myocardial ischemia using routinely acquired non-contrast CTCS scans. Calcium-omics features provided incremental predictive value beyond conventional risk factors and Agatston scoring and may support more accessible cardiovascular risk stratification.

</details>


### [47] [Models Can Model, But Can't Bind: Structured Grounding in Text-to-Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21751)
*Zhiqi Gao,Albert Ge,Alexander Berenbeim,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了BIND方法，通过将数值数据外部化到结构化文件来解决文本到优化中的有效绑定限制问题，将GPT-5-Nano的准确率从59.1%提升至82.4%，GPT-5从86.2%提升至95.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到优化任务面临"有效绑定限制"问题：随着实例数据规模增长，模型准确率急剧下降，即使优化问题本身结构简单。这是因为模型需要两个分离能力：建模（选择正确的优化结构）和绑定（将系数、索引和参数与具体问题数据关联），而后者在数据量大时表现糟糕。

Method: 提出BIND推理时方法：将数值数据外部化到结构化文件中，使模型通过程序化方式绑定数据而非从提示中转录。同时构建Text2Opt-Bench基准，包含12个类别、经求解器验证的优化问题，涵盖从教科书线性规划到随机和多目标规划，变量数量达数千个。

Result: BIND方法显著提升性能：GPT-5-Nano准确率从59.1%升至82.4%（以更低token成本达到pass@5的82.0%水平），GPT-5从86.2%升至95.8%。专门针对绑定任务微调的1.5B参数模型在三个结构不同的优化类别上超越端到端SFT和RL方法，甚至匹配7B参数的端到端基线。

Conclusion: 建模和绑定是两个可分离的能力，应该分别处理。通过将数值绑定任务外部化，可以有效突破有效绑定限制。专门化的绑定模型即使参数量较小也能达到或超越大型端到端模型的表现，证明了分离建模与绑定策略的有效性。

Abstract: Text-to-optimization requires two separable capabilities: modeling -- choosing the right optimization structure -- and binding -- grounding every coefficient, index, and parameter in the concrete problem data. We study this via Text2Opt-Bench, a scalable benchmark of solver-verified optimization problems spanning 12 categories, from textbook linear programs to stochastic and multi-objective formulations with up to thousands of variables. Across 10+ models, we find that accuracy collapses as instance data grows, even when the formulation itself is simple. We call this the effective binding limit. We address this via a simple inference-time approach, BIND, which externalizes numeric data to structured files so the model binds data programmatically rather than transcribing from the prompt. BIND improves GPT-5-Nano from 59.1% to 82.4% accuracy, matching pass@5 (82.0%) at lower token cost than pass@1, and GPT-5 from 86.2% to 95.8%. Furthermore, we validate our hypothesis by finetuning a model exclusively on binding and show that it outperforms end-to-end SFT and RL across three structurally distinct optimization categories, with a 1.5B binding specialist alone matching a 7B end-to-end baseline.

</details>


### [48] [PEARL: Unbiased Percentile Estimation via Contrastive Learning for Industrial-Scale Livestream Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21752)
*Blake Gella,Wei Wu,Yuhao Yin,Zexi Huang,Zikai Wang,Emily Liu,Junlin Zhang,Wentao Guo,Qinglei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEARL框架，通过非参数对比百分位近似方法解决推荐系统中的行为强度不平衡问题，在线上直播平台实现了观看时长+2.10%、消费金额+0.80%、互动率+1.49%和举报率-6.91%的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统在用户交互数据上训练时面临行为强度不平衡问题——用户异质性参与模式导致的系统性偏差。这种不平衡使反馈信号失真，观察到交互不再真实反映用户偏好，导致模型过度放大高活跃用户信号而忽略其他用户，最终降低推荐质量和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出非参数对比百分位近似框架PEARL：1）基于相对优势去偏，建模相对偏好信号而非绝对参与强度；2）利用真实对比交互样本直接近似百分位关系，无需辅助分布估计模型；3）提出基于预测的Bootstrap机制进行百分位平滑，处理稀疏离散反馈；4）引入广义价值加权公式和协同训练策略增强建模灵活性和表示学习。

Result: 理论证明成对比较产生百分位偏好信号的无偏估计。离线实验证明PEARL有效缓解行为偏差，在多个排序目标上持续提升性能。在线A/B测试显示显著收益：观看时长+2.10%，消费金额+0.80%，互动率+1.49%，举报率-6.91%，系统已部署于数十亿用户的直播生产平台。

Conclusion: PEARL通过建模相对偏好信号而非绝对参与强度，成功解决了行为强度不平衡问题，为大规模推荐系统提供了有效且鲁棒的去偏方案，兼具理论保证和实际部署价值。

Abstract: Recommender systems trained on user interaction data are susceptible to behavioral intensity imbalance--a systematic distortion arising from heterogeneous engagement patterns across users. This imbalance skews feedback signals such that observed interactions no longer faithfully reflect true preferences, causing models to disproportionately amplify signals from highly active users while underrepresenting others, which ultimately degrades recommendation quality and robustness at scale. To address this issue, we propose a nonparametric contrastive percentile approximation framework, PEARL, that models relative preference signals instead of absolute engagement magnitudes. Building upon relative advantage debiasing, PEARL leverages real contrastive interaction samples to approximate percentile relationships directly, without relying on auxiliary distribution estimation models. We provide theoretical justification demonstrating that such pairwise comparisons yield unbiased estimates of percentile-based preference signals. For broader applicability, we introduce a prediction-based bootstrapping mechanism for percentile smoothing to handle sparse and discrete feedback, alongside a generalized value-weighted formulation and a co-training strategy to enhance both modeling flexibility and representation learning. Extensive offline experiments demonstrate that PEARL effectively mitigates behavioral bias and consistently improves recommendation performance across multiple ranking targets. Deployed in a production livestream platform with a combined user base of billions, online A/B testing confirms substantial real-world gains: +2.10% Watch Duration, +0.80% Consumption Amount, +1.49% Interaction Rate, and -6.91% Report Rate.

</details>


### [49] [Machine learning prediction of obstructive coronary artery disease using opportunistic coronary calcium and epicardial fat assessments from CT calcium scoring scans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21762)
*Juhwan Lee,Ammar Hoori,Tao Hu,Justin N. Kim,Mohamed H. E. Makhlouf,Michelle C. Williams,David E. Newby,Robert Gilkeson,Sanjay Rajagopalan,David L. Wilson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了一个基于CatBoost-SHAP的机器学习框架，利用CTCS图像中的钙化组学和心外膜脂肪组学特征预测阻塞性冠心病，在1,324名患者中实现了83.1%的敏感性和93.8%的特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非对比CT钙化评分(CTCS)是一种经济有效的影像学检查方法，广泛用于检测冠状动脉钙化。本研究旨在开发先进的机器学习框架，利用CTCS图像中冠状动脉钙化和心外膜脂肪的定量分析来预测阻塞性冠状动脉疾病(CAD)，以改善临床决策制定并减少对对比增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖。

Method: 研究纳入SCOT-HEART临床试验中1,324例同时接受CTCS和冠状动脉CTA检查的患者。从CTCS图像中提取并分析了424个候选特征，包括24个临床变量、189个钙化组学和211个心外膜脂肪组学特征。采用CatBoost算法结合SHAP值进行特征选择，使用CatBoost梯度提升方法构建预测模型，筛选最具信息量的特征进行建模。

Result: 通过CatBoost-SHAP方法从424个候选特征中筛选出14个最具预测性的特征，其中前两个预测特征来源于脂肪组学，其余12个特征来源于钙化组学。优化后的模型表现出稳健的预测能力：敏感性83.1±4.6%，特异性93.8±1.7%，准确率85.3±2.0%，F1分数73.9±3.3%。纳入钙化组学和脂肪组学数据显著提高了预测性能。值得注意的是，该模型在不同冠状动脉钙化评分患者中均显示出可靠的预测准确性，包括钙化评分为零但存在阻塞性CAD的病例。

Conclusion: 这种创新方法有望改善临床决策制定，特别是在低至中度风险患者群体中，可能减少对对比增强或侵入性诊断程序的依赖。该研究证明了结合钙化组学和脂肪组学特征在预测阻塞性CAD方面的价值，为零钙化评分患者的风险评估提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Non-contrast computed tomography calcium scoring (CTCS) is a cost-effective imaging modality widely used to detect coronary artery calcifications. This study aimed to develop an advanced machine learning framework that utilizes quantitative analyses of coronary calcium and epicardial fat from CTCS images to predict obstructive coronary artery disease (CAD). The study population consisted of 1,324 patients from the SCOT-HEART clinical trial who underwent both CTCS and coronary CT angiography. We extracted and analyzed a broad range of features, including 24 clinical variables, 189 calcium-omics, and 211 epicardial fat-omics features from the CTCS images. Feature selection was conducted using the CatBoost algorithm combined with SHapley Additive exPlanation (SHAP) values. Predictive modeling utilized the CatBoost gradient boosting method, focusing on the most informative features. From an initial set of 424 candidate features, 14 were identified as most predictive through the CatBoost-SHAP method. The top two predictive features originated from fat-omics, with the remaining 12 features derived from calcium-omics. The optimized model achieved robust predictive capabilities, demonstrating a sensitivity of 83.1+/-4.6%, specificity of 93.8+/-1.7%, accuracy of 85.3+/-2.0%, and an F1 score of 73.9+/-3.3%. Inclusion of calcium-omics and fat-omics data significantly improved predictive performance. Notably, the model also showed reliable predictive accuracy in patients with diverse coronary calcium scores, including cases with obstructive CAD despite a zero-calcium score. This innovative approach holds promise for improving clinical decision-making and potentially reducing dependence on contrast-enhanced or invasive diagnostic procedures, particularly within low-to intermediate-risk patient groups.

</details>


### [50] [On the Sample Complexity of Discounted Reinforcement Learning with Optimized Certainty Equivalents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21763)
*Oliver Mortensen,Mohammad Sadegh Talebi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限折扣MDP中基于优化确定性等价（OCE）风险度量的强化学习，给出了PAC可学习性的充要条件，证明了效用函数定义域非全时问题不可学习，并建立了样本复杂度的紧下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险敏感强化学习在实际决策中至关重要，但现有研究对基于优化确定性等价（OCE）风险度量（包括熵风险、CVaR、均值-方差等）的学习问题样本复杂度缺乏系统性理解，特别是PAC可学习性条件和下界分析。

Method: 1. 考虑具有生成模型的有限折扣MDP，研究递归OCE风险度量下的状态-动作值函数和最优策略学习问题；
2. 采用基于模型的方法，推导PAC样本复杂度上界；
3. 建立效用函数性质与PAC可学习性之间的理论联系，给出充要条件刻画；
4. 构造信息论下界证明方法，建立样本复杂度下界。

Result: 1. 精确刻画了效用函数u的PAC可学习性条件；
2. 证明了当效用函数定义域dom(u)≠ℝ时，对应问题不具备PAC可学习性；
3. 建立了值学习和策略学习的样本复杂度下界，在状态-动作空间尺寸SA上达到紧性；
4. 对受限效用函数类，建立了关于有效视界1/(1-γ)的显式下界；
5. 针对CVaR_τ证明了正确的τ依赖为1/τ²，相比现有最优结果改进了1/τ因子。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了OCE风险度量下强化学习的PAC可学习性问题，提供了可学习性的完整理论刻画和紧样本复杂度界，特别是对CVaR获得了τ依赖的改进结果，为风险敏感强化学习算法设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study risk-sensitive reinforcement learning in finite discounted MDPs, where a generative model of the MDP is assumed to be available. We consider a family or risk measures called the optimized certainty equivalent (OCE), which includes important risk measures such as entropic risk, CVaR, and mean-variance. Our focus is on the sample complexities of learning the optimal state-action value function (value learning) and an optimal policy (policy learning) under recursive OCE. We provide an exact characterization of utility functions $u$ for which the corresponding OCE defines an objective that is PAC-learnable. We analyze a simple model-based approach and derive PAC sample complexity bounds. We establish that whenever $u$ does not have full domain $\text{dom}(u)\neq \mathbb{R}$, the corresponding problem is not PAC-learnable. Finally, we establish corresponding lower bounds for both value and policy learning, demonstrating tightness in the size $SA$ of state-action space, and for a more restricted class of utilities, we derive lower bounds that makes the dependence on the effective horizon $\frac{1}{1-γ}$ explicit. Specifically, for $\text{CVaR}_τ$ we show that the correct dependence on $τ$ is $\frac{1}{τ^2}$, thus improving by a factor of $\frac{1}τ$ over state-of-the-art although our bound has a suboptimal dependence on $\frac{1}{1-γ}$.

</details>


### [51] [Position: The Time for Sampling Is Now! Charting a New Course for Bayesian Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21765)
*Emanuel Sommer,David Rügamer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证基于采样的推理（SAI）在贝叶斯神经网络中已达到与优化方法相当的计算效率，有望成为主流推断范式，需克服认知误区并聚焦后验探索与样本蒸馏两大核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯神经网络中基于采样的推理方法实际应用受限，主要源于对其可行性和效率的持续误解；本文旨在纠正这些误解，推动SAI成为贝叶斯深度学习的核心工具，实现原则性不确定性量化。

Method: 通过立场性论证，系统分析SAI与优化方法的计算对等性，识别当前误区并提出研究方向重定位；聚焦两个核心问题：后验景观的充分探索、后验样本的高保真蒸馏以实现高效下游推理。

Result: 论证了SAI已实现与优化方法的计算对等性，具备超越优化方法的潜力；SAI可通过模型平均实现更优预测性能，为下游任务提供基础，并揭示BNN景观特性。

Conclusion: SAI有望成为贝叶斯深度学习的核心工具，但需社区共同努力：首先克服认知障碍，其次集中攻克后验探索与样本蒸馏的剩余挑战，从而释放SAI的全部潜力。

Abstract: The practical adoption of sampling-based inference (SAI) in Bayesian neural networks (BNNs) remains limited, partly due to persistent misconceptions about the feasibility and efficiency of sampling. This position paper argues that SAI has achieved computational parity with optimization-based methods and is at the verge of superseding such methods for effective and efficient inference in BNNs. This development should be in the interest of the whole community, promoting BNNs as a principled paradigm with its long-standing yet unfulfilled promise of providing principled uncertainty quantification for neural networks. SAI can even do more -- yielding superior prediction performance through model averaging, serving as the foundation for a plethora of possible downstream tasks, and providing crucial insights into the landscape of BNNs. In order to make such a change happen and unfold the potential of sampling, overcoming current misconceptions is a necessary first step. The next step is to realign research efforts toward addressing remaining challenges in SAI. In particular, the community must focus on two core problems: sufficient exploration of the posterior landscape and high-fidelity distillation of posterior samples for efficient downstream inference. By addressing conceptual and practical obstacles, we can unlock the full potential of SAI and establish it as a central tool in Bayesian deep learning.

</details>


### [52] [Manifold-Guided Attention Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21770)
*Ian Li,Kapilesh Guruprasad,Raunak Sengupta,Ninad Satish,Loris D'Antoni,Rose Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MAGS方法，通过监测注意力头激活与正确性流形的偏离，实现轨迹感知的动态激活引导，显著提升LLM在推理任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有激活引导方法使用固定的预计算校正向量，忽略了模型在生成轨迹中的当前位置，导致对正确和错误步骤的无差别扰动；需要一种能够识别并针对性修正错误的方法。

Method: 基于几何观察：特定注意力头的输出激活在错误点偏离低维正确性流形；从正确/错误轨迹对比对中学习低维子空间捕获错误行为偏离方向；推理时监控注意力头与流形的距离，当偏离超过阈值时施加投影校正，将注意力输出引导回正确子空间。

Result: 在数学推理（MATH-500, GSM8K）、代码生成（HumanEval, MBPP）和分子生成（SMILES）基准测试中，MAGS持续优于无引导基线和静态引导方法。

Conclusion: 正确性流形是LLM注意力几何的通用特征；轨迹感知的激活引导方法能够有效识别并修正推理错误，具有跨任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models frequently produce errors in reasoning tasks despite possessing the underlying knowledge required for correct reasoning. One possible approach to improve reasoning consistency is through activation steering. However, existing activation steering approaches apply fixed, pre-computed correction vectors, ignoring where the model currently sits along its generation trajectory; the result is indiscriminate perturbation that disrupts already-correct steps as freely as erroneous ones. We propose Manifold-Guided Attention Steering (MAGS), a trajectory-aware inference-time intervention grounded in a geometric observation: the output activations of specific attention heads diverge from a low-dimensional correctness manifold at the point of error, and this deviation compounds through subsequent steps. For each identified attention head, we learn a low-dimensional subspace from contrastive pairs of correct and incorrect traces that capture the directions along which error behavior deviates from correct behavior. During inference, we monitor each head's proximity to this manifold and apply a targeted projection correction when deviation exceeds a learned threshold, steering the attention output back toward the correct subspace before the error propagates. MAGS consistently outperforms both unsteered baselines and static steering approaches across benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning (MATH-500, GSM8K), code generation (HumanEval, MBPP), and molecular generation (SMILES), suggesting that correctness manifolds are a general feature of LLM attention geometry.

</details>


### [53] [Provable Robustness against Backdoor Attacks via the Primal-Dual Perspective on Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21780)
*Aman Saxena,Jan Schuchardt,Yan Scholten,Stephan Günnemann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于差分隐私对偶视角的随机平滑框架，能够对训练阶段和推理阶段的组合机制提供端到端的鲁棒性认证，有效防御包括后门攻击在内的复杂威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机平滑方法可以分别针对投毒攻击（通过随机化训练）和规避攻击（通过随机化推理）提供鲁棒性认证，但难以将其扩展到后门攻击场景，因为后门攻击中训练数据和测试数据被联合扰动，需要在单一鲁棒性证书内同时分析训练时和推理时的随机化机制。

Method: 将随机平滑与差分隐私的对偶视角通过隐私档案（privacy profiles）联系起来，提供了一种数值程序来组合异构机制。该框架能够对复杂的组合机制进行紧密、模块化、端到端的认证，同时利用现有的差分隐私机制分析。具体实现了DP-SGD和深度分区聚合（Deep Partition Aggregation）结合推理时平滑的方法。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上进行了实验验证，证明了该框架能够针对训练时攻击和推理时攻击推导出联合鲁棒性保证，实验结果表明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个有原则且通用的框架，通过组合机制来认证复杂威胁模型下的鲁棒性，这些威胁模型能更好地捕捉现实世界对手的能力，为防御复杂攻击（如后门攻击）提供了新的理论基础和实践工具。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing is a powerful tool for certifying robustness to adversarial perturbations, including poisoning attacks via randomized training and evasion attacks via randomized inference. Extending these guarantees to backdoor attacks, where training and test data are jointly perturbed, remains challenging because training- and test-time randomized mechanisms must be analyzed within a single robustness certificate. We address this by connecting randomized smoothing to the dual view of differential privacy through privacy profiles, which provide a numerical procedure for composing heterogeneous mechanisms. The resulting framework enables tight, modular, end-to-end certification of complex, composed mechanisms while leveraging existing analyses of differentially private mechanisms. We instantiate the framework for DP-SGD and Deep Partition Aggregation with inference-time smoothing, deriving joint robustness guarantees against both training-time and inference-time attacks. Experiments on MNIST and CIFAR-10 demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework. Overall, we provide a principled and general framework for using composite mechanisms to certify robustness under complex threat models that better capture the capabilities of real-world adversaries.

</details>


### [54] [MMD-Balls as Credal Sets: A PAC-Bayesian Framework for Epistemic Uncertainty in Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21783)
*Ahanaf Hasan Ariq*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PAC-Bayesian的测试时适应（TTA）理论框架，利用最大均值差异（MMD）量化分布偏移，并首次将其解释为Walley不精确概率理论中的信任集，实现了对认知不确定性的形式化量化与适应决策的理论指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时适应（TTA）方法虽能提升分布偏移下的模型性能，但缺乏形式化保证来关联偏移幅度与预测可靠性。因此亟需建立理论框架，将分布偏移程度显式参数化并融入泛化界限中，从而量化不确定性并判断何时进行适应。

Method: 1. 建立PAC-Bayesian框架，引入最大均值差异（MMD）作为源分布与目标分布间距离度量，在RKHS-Lipschitz损失假设下推导带MMD依赖偏移惩罚的泛化界；2. 利用MMD集中不等式给出有限样本版本；3. 将源分布周围的MMD球解释为Walley不精确概率理论中的信任集（credal sets），在该集合上推导一致最坏风险界及上下风险分解；4. 提出测地线保持界，解释核引导适应为何能保护局部特征几何结构。

Result: 获得了显式依赖MMD的PAC-Bayesian泛化界与有限样本版本；建立了信任集上的一致最坏风险界，实现了认知不确定性与随机不确定性的分离；给出了核引导适应保护局部特征几何的理论解释；提供了适应是否值得进行的决策准则。

Conclusion: 本框架首次将MMD参数化的分布偏移与不精确概率理论结合，为TTA提供了可证明的泛化保证与不确定性量化方法，使适应决策具有理论依据，并为理解核方法在适应中的有效性提供了几何视角。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) methods improve model performance under distribution shift but lack formal guarantees connecting shift magnitude to prediction reliability. We develop a PAC-Bayesian framework yielding generalization bounds explicitly parameterized by the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) between source and target distributions. Our principal contribution is interpreting MMD-balls around the source distribution as credal sets in Walley's imprecise probability theory, yielding natural epistemic uncertainty quantification. We establish: (i) a PAC-Bayesian bound with an MMD-dependent shift penalty under an RKHS-Lipschitz loss assumption; (ii) a finite-sample version via MMD concentration; (iii) a uniform worst-case risk bound over all distributions in the credal set, with a lower-upper risk decomposition; and (iv) geodesic preservation bounds explaining why kernel-guided adaptation protects local feature geometry. The credal set interpretation separates epistemic from aleatoric uncertainty and provides a principled decision criterion for when adaptation is warranted.

</details>


### [55] [Three Costs of Amortizing Gaussian Process Inference with Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21798)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了潜在神经过程(LNP)近似高斯过程(GP)推断时的KL散度上界，将其分解为标签污染、信息瓶颈和摊销误差三个可解释来源，并给出了瓶颈项随表示维度d的衰减速率以及架构设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经过程通过学习到的O(n)映射替代GP精确推断的O(n³)后验，实现了摊销推断，但其预测分布与GP预测分布之间的差异缺乏理论刻画。理解这种近似误差的来源和性质对于改进架构设计至关重要。

Method: 针对一类潜在神经过程，推导了GP预测分布与LNP预测分布之间KL散度的上界，将其分解为三个可解释项：标签污染(使用标签值估计GP中标签无关的量)、信息瓶颈(有限维表示无法解析完整上下文几何)、摊销误差(单一编码器网络共享于所有上下文)。

Result: 对于平方指数核，瓶颈截断项按O(e^{-cd^{2/d_x}})衰减；对于Matérn-ν核按O(d^{-2ν/d_x})衰减，直接将架构尺寸与核平滑度及输入维度关联。标签污染项通常为O(1)，仅观测噪声部分按O(1/n)衰减，揭示了通过标签依赖表示进行不确定性估计的持续代价。

Conclusion: 这些结果刻画了所分析类别内摊销的代价，提出了架构建议：在GP摊销机制下仅从上下文位置预测方差，以及用二阶池化替代均值聚合来消除主导的摊销差距。

Abstract: Neural processes amortize Gaussian process inference, replacing the exact $O(n^3)$ posterior with a learned $O(n)$ map from context sets to predictive distributions. For a class of latent neural processes, we bound the Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence between the GP and LNP predictives, decomposing it into three interpretable sources, namely label contamination as the neural process uses label values to estimate a quantity that is label-independent in the exact GP, an information bottleneck because the finite-dimensional representation cannot resolve the full context geometry, and amortization error from a single encoder network shared across all contexts. The bottleneck truncation term decays in the representation dimension $d$ as $O(e^{-cd^{2/d_x}})$ for squared-exponential kernels on $\mathbb{R}^{d_x}$ where $c > 0$ is a kernel-dependent constant and as $O(d^{-2ν/d_x})$ for Matérn-$ν$ kernels, directly linking architecture sizing to kernel smoothness and input dimension. The label contamination term is $O(1)$ in general, with only the observation-noise component decaying as $O(1/n)$, identifying a persistent cost of routing uncertainty estimation through a label-dependent representation. These results characterize the costs of amortization within the analyzed class and yield architectural recommendations to predict variance from context locations alone in the GP-amortization regime, and replace mean aggregation with second-order pooling to close the dominant amortization gap.

</details>


### [56] [stable-worldmodel: A Platform for Reproducible World Modeling Research and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21800)
*Lucas Maes,Quentin Le Lidec,Luiz Facury,Nassim Massaudi,Ayush Chaurasia,Francesco Capuano,Richard Gao,Taj Gillin,Dan Haramati,Damien Scieur,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了stable-worldmodel(swm)，一个开源的世界模型标准化研究和评估平台，解决了当前研究中存在的代码库分散、数据加载缓慢和缺乏标准化基准等问题，提供了高性能数据层、现代基线模型实现和丰富的评估环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究面临三个关键瓶颈：代码库脆弱且一次性、视频数据加载速度慢、缺乏标准化的泛化基准测试，这些因素阻碍了研究的可复现性和公平比较，导致研究进展缓慢且难以验证。

Method: 构建了stable-worldmodel(swm)开源平台，包含三个核心组件：(1)基于Lance的高性能数据层，原生支持MP4、HDF5和LeRobot数据集及转换工具；(2)现代世界模型基线和规划求解器的清晰、经过充分测试的实现；(3)扩展的环境和任务套件，具有可控的视觉、几何和物理变化因子。

Result: swm平台成功统一了完整的研究流程，在单一可扩展框架下提供了高性能数据处理、标准化的模型实现和系统化的评估能力，大幅降低了研究开销，支持对动力学理解、控制性能、表示质量和分布外泛化进行系统性评估。

Conclusion: 通过提供一个标准化、可复现的世界模型研究和评估平台，swm显著减少了研究开销，加速了朝向可靠世界模型的可信研究进展，为该领域的未来发展奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: World models are central to building agents that can reason, plan, and generalize beyond their training data. However, research on world models is currently fragmented, with disparate codebases, data pipelines, and evaluation protocols hindering reproducibility and fair comparison. Current practice is further limited by three key bottlenecks: fragile one-off codebases, slow video data loading, and the lack of standardized generalization benchmarks. We present stable-worldmodel (swm), an open-source platform for standardized and reproducible world modeling research and evaluation. It delivers (1) a high-performance Lance-based data layer with native support and conversion tools for MP4, HDF5, and LeRobot datasets, (2) clean, well-tested implementations of modern world model baselines and planning solvers, and (3) a broad suite of environments and tasks extended with controllable visual, geometric, and physical factors of variation for systematic in-silico evaluation of dynamics understanding, control performance, representation quality, and out-of-distribution generalization. By unifying the full pipeline under a single, scalable framework, \texttt{swm} dramatically reduces research overhead and accelerates trustworthy progress toward reliable world models.

</details>


### [57] [Why Semantic Entropy Fails: Geometry-Aware and Calibrated Uncertainty for Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21801)
*Zheyuan Zhang,Kaiwen Shi,Han Bao,Zehong Wang,Tianyi Ma,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了GCPO框架，通过几何感知度量捕捉语义分歧并结合基于奖励的校准，首次从理论上将不确定性信号解释为调节梯度方差和学习信号质量的机制，有效提升了大语言模型后训练性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无评论家模型的后训练方法缺乏区分信息性和噪声信号的机制，现有基于熵的不确定性估计器在调节GRPO等优化方法时表现不稳定，对优化动态的影响尚不清晰。

Method: 提出Geometric-aware Calibrated Policy Optimization (GCPO)框架，整合几何感知度量捕捉语义分歧，利用基于奖励的校准使不确定性与学习信号强度对齐，从而更准确地刻画和调节梯度方差。

Result: 识别了当前基于熵的估计器存在各向异性差距和校准差距两个关键问题；在多个基准测试中，GCPO更忠实地跟踪梯度变异性，并持续提升后训练性能。

Conclusion: 设计不确定性信号时需与优化动态对齐，这为鲁棒的后训练提供了有原则的视角，突出了不确定性信号设计对大语言模型优化的重要性。

Abstract: Post-training has become central to improving reasoning and alignment in large language models, where critic-free models enable scalable learning from model-generated outputs but lack principled mechanisms to distinguish informative from noisy signals. Recent approaches leverage response-level measures as uncertainty signals to regulate group-based optimization methods such as GRPO. Yet their empirical success remains unstable and unclear in how they influence optimization dynamics. In this paper, we provide, to our knowledge, the first principled formulation that interprets uncertainty signals as mechanisms for characterizing and regulating gradient variance and learning signal quality. Based on both empirical and theoretical analysis, we identify two critical gaps of current entropy-based estimators: The anisotropic gap and The calibration gap. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Geometric-aware Calibrated Policy Optimization (GCPO), a novel framework integrating geometry-aware measures to capture semantic disagreement with reward-based calibration to align uncertainty with learning signal strength. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that our approach more faithfully tracks gradient variability and consistently improves post-training performance. Our results highlight the importance of designing uncertainty signals that are aligned with optimization dynamics, offering a principled perspective for robust post-training.

</details>


### [58] [Same Architecture, Different Capacity: Optimizer-Induced Spectral Scaling Laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21803)
*Nandan Kumar Jha,Brandon Reagen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 首次证明优化器是表征缩放的第一性轴：Muon在难学样本上实现线性谱容量缩放(β=1.02)，显著优于AdamW(β=0.44)，提升2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律将优化器视为固定训练细节，忽略了优化器如何有效将FFN宽度转化为可用谱容量这一关键维度，这可能错失了表征缩放的基本轴。

Method: 通过软硬谱秩测量FFN表征的本征谱，在固定架构和宽度调度下比较不同优化器(AdamW、Muon)的谱缩放定律，并与架构干预(注意力秩、位置编码)进行对比分析。

Result: Muon在TAIL表征(最难学习区域)实现线性缩放(β=1.02)，AdamW仅弱缩放(β=0.44)；匹配验证损失不保证匹配表征结构；优化器诱导的谱偏移常超过架构效应。

Conclusion: 优化器应被视为表征缩放的第一性轴，不仅影响容量大小，还决定跨特征模的容量结构，这为优化器-架构协同设计提供了理论动机。

Abstract: Scaling laws have made language-model performance predictable from model size, data, and compute, but they typically treat the optimizer as a fixed training detail. We show that this assumption misses a fundamental axis of representation scaling: how effectively the optimizer converts added FFN width into utilized spectral capacity. Using eigenspectra of feed-forward network representations, measured through soft and hard spectral-ranks, we find that \emph{the same Transformer architecture realizes markedly different spectral scaling laws when trained with different optimizers}. Holding architecture and width schedule fixed, AdamW exhibits weak hard-rank scaling ($β$=0.44) on rare-token (TAIL) representations where learning is known to be hardest, whereas Muon achieves linear scaling ($β$=1.02) in the same regimes, a $2.3\times$ increase in the scaling exponent. This difference is not reducible to validation loss: AdamW configurations can match low-rank Dion variants in perplexity, under extended training, while exhibiting sharply different spectral geometry, demonstrating that matched loss does not imply matched representation structure. Hard--soft rank asymmetry further reveals that optimizers differ not only in how much capacity is realized, but also in how that capacity is structured across eigenmodes. To disentangle optimizer effects from architectural ones, we compare against architectural interventions (e.g., attention rank and positional encoding), and find that optimizer-induced spectral shifts often exceed the architectural effects. These results suggest optimization as a first-class axis of representation scaling, motivating optimizer--architecture co-design.

</details>


### [59] [Symbolic Density Estimation for Discrete Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21813)
*Ziwen Liu,Meng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了符号密度估计(SDE)框架，能够自动发现离散概率分布的闭式表达式，在基准测试和实际应用中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可解释离散概率分布目录是通过数百年来逐例数学推导缓慢扩展的，缺乏系统性的自动发现方法。

Method: 提出符号密度估计(SDE)框架，通过在结构化搜索空间中组合基本解析运算自动恢复闭式概率质量函数。该方法结合了领域特定的结构先验、进化搜索和有效性感知推理阶段，并扩展到零膨胀和有限混合等更丰富的分布族。

Result: 在贡献的涵盖常用离散分布的基准数据集上，算法成功恢复了所有基准分布族并提供了准确的参数估计。实际数据应用表明，该方法能识别出简洁且可解释的混合模型，在拟合优度上优于标准模型。

Conclusion: SDE框架为离散概率分布的自动发现提供了有效的解决方案，有助于推动统计建模的发展，并为未来研究提供了系统的评估基准。

Abstract: Discrete probability laws underpin statistical modeling, yet the catalog of interpretable distributions has expanded only gradually through centuries of case-by-case mathematical derivations. We introduce symbolic density estimation (SDE), an unsupervised framework that automatically recovers closed-form probability mass functions by composing elementary analytic operations within a structured search space. Our method integrates domain-specific structural priors with evolutionary search and a validity-aware inference stage, and it extends to richer distribution families such as zero inflation and finite mixtures. To support systematic evaluation and future research, we contribute a benchmark dataset spanning a broad collection of commonly used discrete distributions. The proposed algorithm recovers all benchmark families with accurate parameter estimates. A real data application shows that it identifies concise and interpretable mixture models that improve goodness-of-fit over standard models.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Scalar Objectives: Expert-Feedback-Driven Autonomous Experimentation for Scientific Discovery at the Nanoscale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21820)
*Ralph Bulanadi,Jefferey Baxter,Arpan Biswas,Hiroshi Funakubo,Dennis Meier,Jan Schultheiß,Rama Vasudevan,Yongtao Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了深度核成对学习(DKPL)方法，通过将专家判断融入主动学习循环，解决了自驱动实验室中难以定义标量目标函数的问题，实现了专家知识引导的自主显微实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的贝叶斯优化框架依赖于预定义的标量描述符来指导实验，但在许多情况下难以确定合适的标量描述符，且无法捕捉需要跨学科洞察力的微妙但重要的科学现象。

Method: 开发深度核成对学习(DKPL)方法，让专家直接利用跨学科知识评估实验输出的优劣，而非依赖明确的标量目标。DKPL从这些专家判断中学习潜在效用函数，进而指导后续的自主显微实验。

Result: 在已知真值的实验模型数据集上，DKPL能够学习物理意义的纳米结构并有效优先测量高信息区域；在铁电畴壁分析中，成功区分铋铁氧体中的高低特征畴壁角度，并发现铒锰氧化物中的头对头和尾对尾畴壁特征。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一种将专家知识整合到自主显微实验中的方法，为开发能够解决超越标量指标学习限制的科学问题的专家引导自驱动实验室开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories or autonomous experimentation are emerging as transformative platforms for accelerating scientific discovery. Bayesian optimization (BO) is among the most widely used machine learning frameworks for these purposes, but these BO-based frameworks rely on predefined scalar descriptors to guide experimentation. In many situations, the determination of an appropriate scalar descriptor can be challenging, and may fail to capture subtle yet scientifically important phenomena apparent to experts with interdisciplinary insight. To overcome this limitation, here we develop deep-kernel pairwise learning (DKPL), an approach for autonomous microscopy experiments which incorporates human expertise and interdisciplinary scientific knowledge into an active learning loop. Instead of relying on explicit scalar objectives, DKPL enables experts to directly evaluate which experimental output is more promising using interdisciplinary knowledge. DKPL then learns a latent utility function from these expert judgements to guide subsequent autonomous microscopy experiments. We demonstrate DKPL's performance in learning physically meaningful nanoscale structures while effectively prioritizing high-information measurement regions using an experimental model dataset with known ground truth. We further apply DKPL to analyze the character of ferroelectric domain walls, where we find DKPL capable of distinguishing between high and low characteristic domain-wall angles in bismuth ferrite, and able to discover both head-to-head and tail-to-tail domain-wall character in erbium manganite. This development establishes an approach to integrate expert knowledge into autonomous microscopy experiments and demonstrates a pathway toward expert-guided self-driving laboratories capable of addressing scientific problems beyond the limits of scalar-metrics-driven learning.

</details>


### [61] [On-Policy Consistency Training Improves LLM Safety with Minimal Capability Degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21834)
*Andy Han,Kristina Fujimoto,Avidan Shah,Kiet Nguyen,Kai Xu,Chen Yueh-Han,Ilia Sucholutsky,Rico Angell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在线策略一致性训练(OPCT)，通过模型自身响应计算目标函数来解决现有离线一致性训练泛化差和能力退化问题，在安全性指标上显著优于传统SFT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐模型存在谄媚性、易受越狱攻击、缺乏安全警告等问题。现有的一致性训练方法采用离线生成监督信号并使用SFT微调，导致模型仅记忆训练分布的表面形式，泛化能力差且能力退化严重。

Method: 提出在线策略一致性训练(OPCT)：在模型自身对提示词的响应上计算目标函数，通过模型在对应对比提示词条件下的输出来监督自己，实现在线策略的一致性训练。

Result: 在三个模型家族上评估三个安全维度：(1)谄媚率降至8.1%(基线15.4%，SFT为11.2%)；(2)在自适应攻击下越狱防御成功率接近99%(SFT为87%)；(3)安全意识在2/3模型上优于SFT；(4)避免了SFT导致的能力退化(如MATH-500下降28分)。

Conclusion: 一致性训练应实现在线策略(OPCT)而非离线SFT形式，特别是在需要超出训练分布泛化的场景下。OPCT在安全性和能力保持方面均表现更优。

Abstract: Aligned models can misbehave in several ways: they are often sycophantic, fall victim to jailbreaks, or fail to include appropriate safety warnings. Consistency training is a promising new alignment paradigm to mitigate such failures by training invariants into the model using contrastive input pairs. Existing consistency training procedures generate the supervision signal once, offline, and use supervised fine-tuning (SFT) to update the model. Unfortunately, the resulting models tend to merely memorize the surface forms of the training distribution and thus generalize poorly and regress in their capabilities. We introduce On-Policy Consistency Training (OPCT), a new consistency training approach where the objective is computed over the model's own responses to prompts, supervised by itself conditioned on corresponding contrastive prompts. We evaluate OPCT on three safety axes: sycophancy, jailbreaking, and safety awareness. Across three model families, OPCT outperforms its SFT counterpart on all safety desiderata. It nearly halves the sycophancy rate relative to baseline (8.1% vs. 15.4%, compared to 11.2% for SFT). Under an adaptive per-target attacker, OPCT holds jailbreak defense success near 99% on held-out jailbreak behaviors, whereas SFT achieves 87% on average. On safety awareness, OPCT outperforms SFT in two out of three models, and matches it on the other. OPCT also largely avoids the capability regressions that SFT induces, such as a 28-point drop on MATH-500. Our results suggest that consistency training is best implemented as OPCT rather than as SFT, especially when generalization beyond the training distribution is desired.

</details>


### [62] [Energy-Gated Attention: Spectral Salience as an Inductive Bias for Transformer Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21842)
*Athanasios Zeris*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出能量门控注意力机制(EGA)，通过键词元嵌入的谱能量门控值聚合，在几乎无额外计算成本下显著降低验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer注意力机制平等对待所有词元，忽略了其内在信息含量的差异。受湍流流体动力学中相干结构(携带主要能量并主导传输)启发，作者认为Transformer注意力中应存在类似机制：信息密集型词元(形态边界、句法中心、话语标记)应比背景词元(功能词、重复模式)获得更多注意力。

Method: 提出能量门控注意力(EGA)：通过单一可学习的线性投影计算键词元嵌入的谱能量，以此门控值聚合过程。该投影自动发现嵌入场的主导谱模式。方法仅增加12,480个参数(<0.26%开销)，无额外计算成本。通过三种小波族(Morlet、Daubechies db2/db4、参数化Morlet)的系统消融实验，验证数据自适应的非正弦最优能量方向。

Result: TinyShakespeare数据集上验证损失提升+0.103，Penn Treebank上提升+0.101，证明方法的数据集无关性。学习到的能量阈值收敛至τ≈0.35，与英语文本中内容词占比(~36%)一致，表明该方法成功识别了稳定的语言特性。消融实验表明固定结构基次优，最优能量方向是数据自适应且非正弦的。

Conclusion: EGA成功将流体动力学中的相干结构理论应用于Transformer注意力机制，证明了信息密集型词元应获得更多注意力的假设。方法在极低参数开销下实现显著性能提升，且能量阈值的一致收敛揭示了深层的语言学规律。学习型小波包被识别为有前景的开放研究方向。

Abstract: Standard transformer attention computes pairwise similarity between queries and keys, treating all tokens as equally salient regardless of their intrinsic informational content. In turbulent fluid dynamics, coherent structures -- the energetically dominant, spatially organized patterns that persist amid background chaos -- carry a disproportionate fraction of total energy and govern all transport. We propose that tokens play an analogous role in transformer attention: informationally dense positions (morphological boundaries, syntactic heads, discourse markers) concentrate spectral energy and should attract proportionally more attention than background tokens (function words, repeated patterns, low-information filler). We propose Energy-Gated Attention (EGA): a simple modification that gates value aggregation by the spectral energy of key token embeddings, computed by a single learned linear projection that discovers the dominant spectral mode of the embedding field. On TinyShakespeare, EGA achieves +0.103 validation loss improvement with only 12,480 additional parameters (<0.26% overhead) and no measurable computational cost. The result is consistent on Penn Treebank (+0.101), demonstrating dataset independence. A systematic ablation across three wavelet families (fixed Morlet, Daubechies db2/db4, and a parametric Morlet) establishes that fixed structured bases are suboptimal -- the optimal energy direction is data-adaptive and non-sinusoidal -- while identifying learned wavelet packets as a promising open direction. The learned energy threshold converges to tau ~= 0.35 independently of initialization, corresponding to the fraction (~36%) of tokens carrying above-average spectral energy in English text, a stable linguistic property consistent with the fraction of content words in running English text.

</details>


### [63] [Geometry-Adaptive Explainer for Faithful Dictionary-Based Interpretability under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21849)
*Sungjun Lim,Heedong Kim,Andrew Lee,Kyungwoo Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出几何自适应解释器(GAE)，通过重新对齐字典解决分布偏移下解释器的忠实度退化问题，仅需无标签OOD激活且无需梯度更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于字典的解释器（如稀疏自编码器和转码器）在分布内(ID)训练，但在分布外(OOD)场景下的忠实度系统研究不足，存在字典与OOD活跃子空间错位问题。

Method: 形式化错位为"忠实度差距"（几何距离），提出GAE方法：利用无标签OOD激活重新对齐字典与OOD活跃子空间，保持原始特征结构，无需梯度更新。

Result: 理论证明GAE优于未适应的ID解释器，过量损失受二阶矩偏移二次上界限制；实证表明GAE在多模型和OOD设置中匹配或超越所有基于训练的基线方法的因果忠实度。

Conclusion: GAE有效填补了忠实度差距，在无需梯度更新的条件下实现了OOD场景下解释器忠实度的显著提升，为机制可解释性提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability aims to explain a model's behavior by identifying causally responsible internal structures. Dictionary-based explainers such as sparse autoencoders and transcoders are a primary tool, but their faithfulness under out-of-distribution (OOD) shift has received little systematic attention. We show that distribution shift rotates the subspace that the model actively uses, misaligning the explainer's dictionary trained on in-distribution (ID) activations. We formalize this misalignment as the faithfulness gap, a geometric distance between the ID dictionary and the OOD-active subspace, and show that it controls OOD faithfulness degradation.
