The core of the proposal is a Variational Autoencoder (VAE) framework built upon the HOPE Block, which replaces quadratic attention with a dual-memory system: Titans modules for dynamic short-term retention and a Continuum Memory System (CMS) for the abstraction of long-term historical context. A key architectural novelty is the disentangled latent space, which factorizes representations into independent trend and seasonal components via dedicated encoder heads and separate decoder pathways. ChronoVAE-HOPE undergoes self-supervised pre-training on the Monash archive, combining a Masked Time Series Modeling (MTSM) auxiliary objective with a disentangled VAE reconstruction loss. The pre-trained encoder is subsequently frozen and used to generate fixed-length embeddings for downstream classification on the UCR benchmark datasets. Empirical results demonstrate strong performance across diverse temporal domains, particularly in settings characterized by strict causal structure. ChronoVAE-HOPE establishes a robust and interpretable framework for the adaptation of foundation models to time series classification through structured generative representations.

</details>


### [170] [Posterior Collapse as Automatic Spectral Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22691)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了β-VAE中的后验坍塌现象实现了自动谱剪枝，通过Landau稳定性分析推导出了潜在模式的级联坍塌行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解β-VAE中后验坍塌现象的物理机制，探究潜在变量如何根据其对重建的贡献被自动选择或剪枝。

Method: 采用Landau稳定性分析方法，定义潜在重缩放不变序参数，在线性高斯情况下验证理论预测，并在WorldClim数据集上进行测试。

Result: 证明了坍塌谱、效用谱和归一化PCA谱在线性高斯情况下重合；每个坍塌遵循平均场定律；不同的β值揭示了潜在模式从最不重要到最重要依次解耦的级联坍塌。

Conclusion: 后验坍塌是一种有益的特征选择机制，β参数设置了贡献阈值，实现了潜在模式的自动谱剪枝，为理解β-VAE的表达能力提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We show that posterior collapse in $β$-VAEs implements automatic spectral pruning. A latent mode collapses if its contribution to reconstruction is below the cutoff set by $β$. Equilibrium solutions with different $β$ thus reveal a cascade of collapses as latent modes decouple from least to most useful.
  We derive this as a consequence of the loss via a Landau stability analysis. We define a latent-rescaling-invariant order parameter that ranks active latent modes and whose collapse thresholds identify which effective variables to inspect first.
  In the linear Gaussian case, the collapse spectrum, utility spectrum, and normalized PCA spectrum coincide, and each collapse follows a mean-field law. We test these predictions on the WorldClim dataset.

</details>


### [171] [Clipping Bottleneck: Stabilizing RLVR via Stochastic Recovery of Near-Boundary Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22703)
*Shuo Yang,Jinda Lu,Chiyu Ma,Kexin Huang,Haoming Meng,Qihui Zhang,Yuyang Liu,Bolin Ding,Guoyin Wang,Li Yuan,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR)方法，通过随机保留边界附近的token来解决RLVR中硬裁剪导致的信息丢失问题，显著提升训练稳定性和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可验证奖励(RLVR)是扩展大语言模型推理的核心范式，但其优化过程常面临训练不稳定和收敛次优的问题。通过对基于裁剪的GRPO风格目标的系统分析，发现硬裁剪决策是关键瓶颈，因为边界附近区域存在有价值的信号，却被标准硬裁剪规则丢弃。

Method: 提出Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR)，一种最小化的即插即用修改方法。该方法在边界处进行随机扰动，以一定概率随机保留略微超出边界的token，从而恢复丢失的信号。虽然这种随机采样在期望上可解释为隐式梯度衰减，但其实验表明随机、局部边界的拯救机制比确定性梯度衰减更有效。

Result: 在7B到30B模型规模以及密集和MoE架构上进行了广泛实验验证。作为即插即用方案，NSR显著改善了训练稳定性，并在DAPO和GSPO等强基线上持续取得性能提升。消融实验证明了随机边界拯救机制比确定性梯度衰减更有效。

Conclusion: 硬裁剪导致的边界附近信息丢失是RLVR训练的关键瓶颈，通过简单的随机边界保留机制可以有效恢复这些信号。NSR作为一种轻量级、即插即用的解决方案，在多种模型规模和架构上都展现出优越的性能和稳定性，为RLVR训练提供了有效的改进思路。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a central paradigm for scaling LLM reasoning, yet its optimization often suffers from training instability and suboptimal convergence. Through a systematic dissection of clipping-based GRPO-style objectives, we identify the rigid clipping decision induced by hard clipping as a key practical bottleneck in the studied RLVR setups. Specifically, our analysis suggests that informative signals can lie in the near-boundary region just beyond the clipping threshold, and are therefore discarded by the standard hard-clipping rule. Notably, once this bottleneck is precisely identified, even simple stochastic perturbations at the boundary can recover meaningful performance gains. Building on this finding, we propose Near-boundary Stochastic Rescue (NSR), a minimal, plug-and-play modification that stochastically retains these slightly out-of-bound tokens to recover lost signals. While NSR, via stochastic sampling, can be interpreted as inducing an implicit gradient decay in expectation, our ablations reveal that its stochastic, boundary-local rescue mechanism is consistently more effective than deterministic gradient decay. Validated by extensive experiments across model sizes from 7B to 30B and both dense and MoE architectures, as a plug-and-play solution, NSR substantially improves training stability and delivers consistent gains over strong baselines such as DAPO and GSPO.

</details>


### [172] [Reading Task Failure Off the Activations: A Sparse-Feature Audit of GPT-2 Small on Indirect Object Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22719)
*Mahdi Nasermoghadasi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可复现的审计流程，用于识别GPT-2 small在间接对象识别（IOI）任务失败时相关的稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征，发现特征17491（'cryptographic keys'）与特定失败模式强相关，但通过因果消融等控制实验证明其仅为相关因素而非充分原因，强调了审计流程本身的方法论价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于开发一种低成本、可复现的方法来识别和解释语言模型在特定任务失败时相关的内部特征表示，以期提高模型可解释性并理解其失败机制。

Method: 在300个提示词上对GPT-2 small的层8残差流SAE特征进行审计，使用Holm校正显著性阈值和Cohen's d效应量筛选失败与成功试验间的差异特征；对最强相关特征进行三项控制测试：(i)因果消融实验；(ii)表示基线对比（logistic回归）；(iii)种子鲁棒性检验。

Result: GPT-2 small在IOI任务上达到79.7%准确率；146个特征通过显著性阈值，105个达到大效应量；特征17491（d=+2.93）与'keys'对象的失败率（93.3%）高度相关；但消融实验未能恢复准确率，SAE特征与原始残差流预测力相当（ROC AUC≈0.93），特征排名在不同种子间不稳定。

Conclusion: 单个SAE特征（如17491）仅为失败的相关因素而非充分原因；研究的核心贡献在于提出的审计流程——低成本、模型无关、可生成可解释的相关特征，而非任何单一发现；该流程可在笔记本上运行，已开源代码和数据。

Abstract: We report a small, reproducible audit of which sparse-autoencoder (SAE) features of GPT-2 small fire differently on failed versus successful trials of the Indirect Object Identification (IOI) task. On 300 prompts, GPT-2 small reaches 79.7% accuracy; 146 of the 24,576 features in the layer-8 residual-stream SAE release of Bloom (2024) clear a Holm-corrected significance threshold and 105 reach a large effect size (|Cohen's d| > 0.8). The strongest single correlate of failure -- feature 17,491, d=+2.93, Neuronpedia label 'cryptographic keys' -- is essentially silent except when the prompt's transferred object is 'the keys,' on which GPT-2 small fails 93.3% of the time vs. 7.5% on the other seven objects (Fisher exact p = 8.79 x 10^-33). We put this correlate through three controls that a mechanistic claim should pass. (i) A causal ablation: zeroing feature 17,491 in the residual stream across all token positions of the 45 keys prompts does not restore accuracy (6.7% -> 4.4%); the feature is a correlate, not a sufficient cause at this layer. (ii) A representation baseline: a logistic regression on the raw 768-dimensional residual stream reaches 5-fold ROC AUC = 0.929, matching the top-100 SAE features (0.927); the SAE basis adds interpretability, not predictive power. (iii) A seed-robustness check: across five random seeds the keys-subset failure rate stays in 75.0--93.3% (the behavioural effect is real), but feature 17,491 is the top-|d| feature in only 1 of 5 runs. The methodological contribution is therefore the audit pipeline (cheap, model-agnostic, surfaces named correlates) rather than any single feature found through it. We release the code, the 300-prompt corpus, the 300x24,576 activation matrix, the ablation and baseline scripts, and the figures. The full pipeline runs on a laptop (Apple M3 Max, no discrete GPU).

</details>


### [173] [Multiple Neural Operators Achieve Near-Optimal Rates for Multi-Task Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22724)
*Adrien Weihs,Hayden Schaeffer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多任务共享表示下的算子学习，证明了多任务算子学习与单任务算子学习具有相同的扩展定律，共享表示不会增加整体成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在共享多任务设置下学习算子集合的近似和统计复杂度，重点关注多神经算子(MNO)架构，旨在理解多任务共享表示是否会增加学习成本。

Method: 针对广泛的Lipschitz多算子映射类别，推导近似和统计泛化的近似最优上界；在下界方面，建立了参数复杂度诅咒并证明了相应的极小极大速率。同时将MNO与基于拼接任务输入的DeepONet多任务扩展进行对比。

Result: 证明了多任务算子学习遵循与单任务算子学习相同的扩展定律，共享表示不会增加整体成本。从最坏情况近似复杂度角度看，MNO与多任务DeepONet扩展满足基本相同的渐近速率。

Conclusion: 多任务共享表示下的算子学习在复杂度方面与单任务学习等价，不同架构（MNO与多任务DeepONet）在最坏情况下具有相似的渐近性能。

Abstract: We study the approximation and statistical complexity of learning collections of operators in a shared multi-task setting, with a focus on the Multiple Neural Operators (MNO) architecture. For broad classes of Lipschitz multiple operator maps, we derive near-optimal upper bounds for approximation and statistical generalization. On the lower-bound side, we establish a curse of parametric complexity and prove corresponding minimax rates. Together, these results show that shared representations across tasks do not increase the overall cost: multi-task operator learning follows the same scaling laws as single operator learning. We also compare MNO with a multi-task extension of DeepONet based on concatenated task inputs and show that, from a worst-case approximation-complexity perspective, both architectures satisfy essentially the same asymptotic rates.

</details>


### [174] [Ternary Decision Trees with Locally-Adaptive Uncertainty Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22740)
*William Smits*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出三元决策树，在分裂节点引入不确定性区域，对边界区域样本进行加权预测和标记，在72个数据集上显著优于传统CART方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统决策树使用硬二值阈值划分特征空间，对远离决策边界和边界上的实例给予相同的置信度，无法区分边界不确定性。

Method: 在每个分裂节点增加半宽度为delta的不确定性区域，区域内实例通过加权混合两个子树的预测结果，并被标记为边界不确定。提出五种delta估计方法：质量平台、类别重叠、增益比率、节点自举和边界距离，仅需标准CART分裂过程中已有的统计信息。

Result: 在72个OpenML-CC18数据集上，五种方法在确定准确率上均显著优于标准CART（p<0.001）。边界距离方法效率最佳（每单位边界不确定标记率获得0.104准确率增益），在42/72数据集上获胜，无需额外超参数。在医学和金融数据集上，节点自举方法在乳腺X光检查中通过标记10.8%筛查病例为边界不确定，实现+0.71%确定准确率。

Conclusion: 三元决策树通过局部不确定性区域有效提升了决策树的预测能力和可解释性，边界距离方法综合表现最优，能够自适应区分边界不确定样本，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Decision trees partition the feature space using hard binary thresholds, assigning identical confidence to instances far from a decision boundary and to those directly on it. We introduce ternary decision trees, which augment each split node with an uncertainty zone of half-width delta centered on the optimal threshold. Instances in this zone receive predictions formed by weighted blending of both child subtrees and are flagged as boundary-uncertain, signaling that downstream applications may treat these predictions differently. Crucially, delta is computed locally at each node from statistics already available during standard CART split finding, requiring no external noise specification. We propose and evaluate five delta-estimation methods: quality-plateau (plateau width of the split criterion curve), class-overlap (empirical class-distribution overlap), gain-ratio (split quality relative to split entropy), node-bootstrap (threshold variance under node-level resampling), and margin (SVM-inspired distance to the nearest cross-class training example). Evaluated across 72 OpenML-CC18 datasets with 5-fold cross-validation, all five methods with probabilistic routing significantly outperform standard CART on decided accuracy (Wilcoxon signed-rank, p < 0.001). The margin method achieves the best efficiency (0.104 accuracy gain per unit of boundary-uncertain flagging rate), wins on 42 of 72 datasets, and requires zero additional hyperparameters. Analysis on three Breiman synthetic benchmarks reveals that margin is self-calibrating on clean data while node-bootstrap and quality-plateau best track theoretical irreducible error. Experiments on four medical and financial datasets demonstrate practical value: on mammography, node-bootstrap achieves +0.71% decided accuracy by flagging 10.8% of screening cases as boundary-uncertain.

</details>


### [175] [SeqLoRA: Bilevel Orthogonal Adaptation for Continual Multi-Concept Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22743)
*Javad Parsa,Enis Simsar,Amir Joudaki,Thomas Hofmann,André M. H. Teixeira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SeqLoRA框架，通过双层优化联合优化LoRA因子，解决了文本到图像扩散模型中多概念组合时的表示干扰问题，在多达101个概念上实现了更好的身份保持和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的参数高效微调方法在组合多个定制概念时面临表示干扰的挑战。现有模块化方法要么依赖昂贵的后验融合，要么冻结适应子空间，这限制了表达能力和概念保真度。

Method: 提出序列正则化LoRA（SeqLoRA），这是一个约束持续学习框架，通过双层优化联合优化两个LoRA因子。理论上将残差层激活建模为矩阵次高斯过程，推导出灾难性遗忘的高概率界限，并证明从数据学习LoRA基比冻结基方法更有效地最小化残差干扰能量。

Result: 在多概念图像生成实验中，SeqLoRA在多达101个概念上提高了身份保持和可扩展性，同时避免了昂贵的融合过程并减少了组合生成中的属性干扰。

Conclusion: SeqLoRA成功解决了参数高效微调中多概念组合的表达能力与概念保真度之间的权衡问题，为文本到图像扩散模型的个性化提供了可扩展且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables fast personalization of text-to-image diffusion models, but composing multiple custom concepts remains challenging due to representation interference. Existing modular methods either rely on expensive post-hoc fusion or freeze adaptation subspaces, which limit expressiveness and concept fidelity. To address this trade-off, we propose Sequential regularized LoRA (SeqLoRA), a constrained continual learning framework that jointly optimizes both LoRA factors via bilevel optimization. Theoretically, we establish strong convergence guarantees for our algorithm and model the residual layer activations as a matrix sub-Gaussian process to derive high-probability bounds on catastrophic forgetting. We further prove that learning the LoRA basis from data minimizes residual interference energy more effectively than frozen-basis methods. Experiments on multi-concept image generation demonstrate that SeqLoRA improves identity preservation and scalability across up to 101 concepts, while avoiding costly fusion and reducing attribute interference in composed generations.

</details>


### [176] [Plug-in Losses for Evidential Deep Learning: A Simplified Framework for Uncertainty Estimation that Includes the Softmax Classifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22746)
*Berk Hayta,Hannah Laus,Simon Mittermaier,Felix Krahmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简化的证据深度学习目标函数，通过在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失来近似原始目标，在保持预测精度和选择性预测性能的同时简化了实现，并首次在语音识别任务中获得了覆盖-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中基于传感器的学习系统需要既可靠又计算高效的不确定性估计。证据深度学习(EDL)通过Dirichlet分布建模类概率提供单次传递的不确定性估计，但Dirichlet期望目标比标准监督学习损失更复杂，给分析和实现带来挑战。

Method: 通过在Dirichlet均值处评估的插件损失来近似EDL诱导的一阶经验风险最小化问题的目标函数。在温和假设下，对于包括均方误差和交叉熵损失在内的广泛损失函数类别，证明近似误差随证据增长而衰减。该框架在特定证据到Dirichlet映射下包含标准softmax分类器。

Result: 在Google Speech Commands数据集上验证了所提出的简化目标函数。实验结果表明，简化方法在预测精度和选择性预测性能方面与经典EDL相当，同时可以使用标准深度学习损失和训练流程更简单地实现。首次在语音识别任务中通过EDL获得覆盖-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 该工作为softmax在不确定性估计背景下的使用提供了理论依据，简化了EDL的实现复杂性，同时保持了其不确定性估计的有效性，为语音识别等实际应用提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Real-world sensor-based learning systems require uncertainty estimation that is both reliable and computationally efficient. Evidential Deep Learning (EDL) provides single-pass uncertainty estimation by modeling the class probabilities via Dirichlet distributions, where the Dirichlet parameters are predicted by a learned neural network mapping. However, this approach can lead to computational challenges, as Dirichlet expected objectives are more complex than standard supervised learning losses, complicating their analysis and implementation. We address this issue by approximating the objective of the first-order empirical risk minimization problem induced by EDL with a plug-in loss evaluated at the Dirichlet mean and show that, under mild assumptions, the approximation error decays with growing evidence for a broad class of loss functions, including mean-squared error and cross-entropy loss. As a special case, our analysis provides justification for the use of softmax in the context of uncertainty estimation, since under a particular evidence-to-Dirichlet mapping, our framework includes the standard softmax classifier. We validate the proposed simplified objectives on the Google Speech Commands dataset and show that they achieve predictive accuracy and selective prediction performance comparable to classical EDL, while being simpler to implement using standard deep learning losses and training pipelines. To the best of our knowledge, this empirical analysis is the first to obtain coverage-accuracy trade-offs for speech recognition tasks through EDL.

</details>


### [177] [Lumberjack: Better Differentially Private Random Forests through Heavy Hitter Detection in Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22756)
*Christian Janos Lebeda,David Erb,Tudor Cebere,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Lumberjack，一种差分隐私随机森林算法，通过构建大型随机决策树并应用激进的隐私保护剪枝策略，在基准数据集上显著优于现有方法，建立了新的最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机森林在涉及敏感表格数据的领域被广泛使用，但现有的差分隐私（DP）方法通常会严重降低性能，以至于在实际应用中不可行。研究动机在于开发一种能够在隐私约束下实现更高实用性的差分隐私随机森林算法。

Method: 提出了Lumberjack算法，该方法构建大型随机决策树，然后应用激进的隐私保护剪枝策略，仅保留足够密集的节点。关键创新是开发了一种用于层次化数据的新型$(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP重击检测算法，其误差为$O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$，使得可以使用比先前工作更深层的树，从而在隐私约束下提高表达能力。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实证评估显示，Lumberjack始终优于先前的DP随机森林方法，建立了新的最先进水平。特别是在实用的隐私预算下，该方法在隐私-效用权衡方面取得了实质性改进。

Conclusion: 研究发现，精心设计的DP随机森林可以缩小大部分效用差距，为未来的研究突出了一个有前景且未被充分探索的方向。这表明在隐私约束下，随机森林算法仍具有巨大的潜力和改进空间。

Abstract: Random forests are widely used in fields involving sensitive tabular data, but existing approaches to enforcing differential privacy (DP) typically degrade performance to the point of impracticality. In this paper, we introduce Lumberjack, a differentially private random forest algorithm that achieves substantially higher utility by constructing large random decision trees and then applying aggressive, privacy-preserving pruning to retain only sufficiently populated nodes. A key component of our approach is a novel $(\varepsilon,δ)$-DP heavy hitter detection algorithm for hierarchical data, whose error is $O_{\varepsilon,δ}(\sqrt{\log h})$ for trees of height $h$ and may be of independent interest. This favorable scaling enables the use of significantly deeper trees than in prior work, leading to improved expressiveness under privacy constraints. Our empirical evaluation on benchmark datasets shows that Lumberjack consistently outperforms prior DP random forest methods, establishing a new state of the art. In particular, our approach yields substantial improvements in the privacy-utility trade-off for practical privacy budgets. Our findings suggest that carefully designed DP random forests can close much of the utility gap, highlighting a promising and underexplored direction for future research.

</details>


### [178] [Uniform Diffusion Models Revisited: Leave-One-Out Denoiser and Absorbing State Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22765)
*Samson Gourevitch,Yazid Janati,Dario Shariatian,Umut Simsekli,Eric Moulines,Eric P. Xing,Alain Durmus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了均匀扩散模型(UDM)中标准插件桥参数化与去噪目标的不匹配问题，提出了留一法后验参数化来解决该问题，并引入吸收态重构来统一UDM与掩码扩散模型的采样机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型通常通过干净数据预测来训练，但反向动力学的定义方式存在不同选择。掩码扩散模型(MDM)中这些选择基本一致，但均匀扩散模型(UDM)中却不一致。作者发现UDM的标准插件桥参数化并未被去噪后验优化，而是被留一法后验优化，这导致了插件ELBO与常用交叉熵去噪目标之间的不匹配。

Method: 1) 推导了留一法目标，建立了去噪器、留一法后验和分数之间的精确转换关系，实现了参数化与训练目标的解耦；2) 提出了一种信息化的预测器-校正器采样器和基于留一法预测器的改进温度采样方法；3) 引入了均匀扩散的吸收态重构，在保持UDM联合分布的同时将其分解为类似掩码扩散的采样操作，简化了去噪后验并提供了自然的重掩码机制。

Result: 留一法参数化在语言建模任务上持续改善了UDM的生成质量；吸收态构造匹配或超越了掩码扩散的性能；无需额外训练即可通过改进的采样器提升推理效果。实验表明掩码扩散与均匀扩散之间的经验差距主要由参数化和采样设计驱动，而非边际分布本身的选择。

Conclusion: 通过正确的参数化和采样设计，均匀扩散模型可以达到与掩码扩散模型相当甚至更优的性能。研究为离散扩散模型的理论理解和实践应用提供了新的见解，代码和模型已开源。

Abstract: Discrete diffusion models are often trained through clean-data prediction, but the prediction can be used in different ways to define the reverse dynamics. In Masked Diffusion Models (MDM) these choices largely coincide, whereas in Uniform Diffusion Models (UDM) they do not. We show that the standard plug-in bridge parameterization for UDM is not optimized by the denoising posterior, but by a leave-one-out posterior that predicts each clean token without using its own noisy observation. This identifies a mismatch between the plug-in ELBO and the usual cross-entropy denoising objective. We characterize the leave-one-out target and derive exact conversions between the denoiser, the leave-one-out posterior, and the score. These conversions allow us to disentangle parameterization and training objective. Our results also lead to inference improvements without any additional training through an informed predictor-corrector sampler and improved temperature sampling based on the leave-one-out predictor.
  We further introduce an absorbing-state reformulation of uniform diffusion that preserves the UDM joint law while decomposing it into masked-diffusion-like sampling operations, with simpler denoising posteriors, carry-over unmasking, and a natural remasking mechanism. On language modeling, leave-one-out parameterizations consistently improve UDM generation, while the absorbing construction matches or surpasses masked diffusion. These results suggest that the empirical gap between masked and uniform diffusion is driven less by the choice of marginals themselves than by parameterization and sampling design. The code and models can be found at https://github.com/samsongourevitch/rev_udm.

</details>


### [179] [Remember to be Curious: Episodic Context and Persistent Worlds for 3D Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22814)
*Lily Goli,Justin Kerr,Daniele Reda,Alec Jacobson,Andrea Tagliasacchi,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合在线3D重建和序列模型的好奇心驱动强化学习方法，解决了在复杂3D环境中探索效率低的问题，在多个任务和环境中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有好奇心驱动强化学习方法在复杂、逼真的3D环境中难以有效应用，主要原因是代理容易陷入局部循环，且在重新访问已遗忘状态时仍能获得奖励。作者发现这种失败源于缺乏空间持久性和情景上下文。

Method: 作者提出了两个关键组件：（1）使用在线3D重建作为持久的世界模型，持续更新环境信息；（2）将代理策略参数化为基于RGB观测的序列模型，以维护情景轨迹历史。这种设计在训练时支持有效探索，在部署时仅需RGB帧即可导航。

Result: 在HM3D数据集上仅通过好奇心训练，该代理超越了基于RL的主动建图基线；零样本泛化到Gibson和AI生成世界；在苹果采摘和图像目标导航等下游任务中表现出高效的适应性，超越了从头训练的基线方法。

Conclusion: 有效的好奇心机制需要结合持久的世界模型和具备情景上下文维护能力的代理，该方法为复杂3D环境中的探索和下游任务学习提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Exploration is a prerequisite for learning useful behaviors in sparse-reward, long-horizon tasks, particularly within 3D environments. Curiosity-driven reinforcement learning addresses this via intrinsic rewards derived from the mismatch between the agent's predictive model of the world and reality. However, translating this intrinsic motivation to complex, photorealistic environments remains difficult, as agents can become trapped in local loops and receive fresh rewards for revisiting forgotten states. In this work, we demonstrate that this failure stems from a lack of spatial persistence and episodic context. We show that effective curiosity requires a model of the world that is persistent and continuously updated, paired with an agent that maintains an episodic trajectory history to navigate toward novel regions. We achieve this using an online 3D reconstruction as a persistent model of the world, while the agent policy is parameterized as a sequence model over RGB observations to maintain episodic context. This design enables effective exploration during training while allowing the agent to navigate using solely RGB frames at deployment. Trained purely via curiosity on HM3D, our agent outperforms RL-based active mapping baselines and generalizes zero-shot to Gibson and AI-generated worlds. Our end-to-end policy enables efficient adaptation to downstream tasks, such as apple picking and image-goal navigation, outperforming from-scratch baselines. Please see video results at https://recuriosity.github.io/.

</details>


### [180] [Integrable Elasticity via Neural Demand Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22820)
*Carlos Heredia,Daniel Roncel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ICDN模型，一种多产品零售需求的神经模型，通过学习对数需求作为对数价格的平滑函数来获得精确的弹性估计，在啤酒数据集上表现优于基准模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统需求模型在估计弱识别的交叉价格效应时存在稳定性差和经济合理性不足的问题。研究者希望建立一个能够从需求曲面中精确推导弹性，并产生经济上合理的弹性估计的模型。

Method: 提出可积上下文相关需求网络（ICDN），这是一种需求优先的神经模型。该模型将对数需求学习为对数价格的平滑、受上下文调节的函数，使得弹性可以精确地从学习的需求曲面中推导出来。

Result: 在Dominick's啤酒数据集上，ICDN在样本外泛化方面优于有向对数-对数基准模型，并产生更稳定、经济上更合理的弹性估计，特别是在弱识别的交叉价格效应方面表现突出。

Conclusion: ICDN模型成功地将机器学习方法与经济学需求理论相结合，为多产品零售需求建模提供了一个既能改善预测性能又能产生经济可解释结果的框架。

Abstract: We propose the Integrable Context-Dependent Demand Network (ICDN), a demand-first neural model for multiproduct retail demand. The model learns log-demand as a smooth, context-conditioned function of log-prices, allowing elasticities to be derived exactly from the learned demand surface. On the Dominick's beer dataset, ICDN improves out-of-sample generalization over a directed log-log benchmark and yields more stable, economically plausible elasticity estimates, especially for weakly identified cross-price effects.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Argo: Efficient Importance Labeling for Enterprise Email Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21604)
*Siddhant Ray,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Kevin Chian,Yan Guo,Cristina St Hill,Jack W. Stokes,Victor Wang,Junchen Jiang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Argo框架，通过权衡成本与质量，用高效替代方案替代昂贵的GPT-4等大语言模型，实现企业级邮件重要性标注的成本大幅降低（推理成本降低148-167倍）且几乎不损失标注质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统邮件重要性标注方法（如关键词匹配、用户规则、发送者启发式）需要大量人工特征工程，难以扩展和泛化；而直接使用GPT-4等大语言模型虽效果优异，但在企业级邮件规模下计算成本过高，无法实际部署。因此需要在标注质量与成本之间找到平衡。

Method: 开发了Argo企业邮件标注框架，包含两个核心组件：(1)构建一个profiler高效搜索标注的成本-质量权衡空间，识别成本高效的替代标注方案；(2)设计按需配置方案，智能地根据实时负载扩展Argo，以最小化峰值负载推理时的成本增加。

Result: 在3个开源邮件数据集上，Argo实现了148-167倍的推理成本降低，同时质量损失可忽略不计；profiling成本降低20-640000倍，使大规模、上下文感知的邮件标注在企业中变得可行。

Conclusion: Argo框架成功解决了企业级邮件重要性标注中成本与质量的权衡问题，证明了通过智能替代方案可以以极低成本达到接近GPT级别的标注质量，为企业实际部署大规模邮件标注系统提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Email importance labeling has long been a critical yet challenging problem for businesses and individuals. Traditional approaches; such as keyword matching, user-defined rules, and sender-based heuristics; demand extensive manual feature engineering and fail to scale effectively or generalize. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong potential and a natural fit for this task, offering deep contextual understanding and superior labeling quality. However, using LLM models like GPT-4.1 at enterprise email volumes incurs prohibitive computational costs and hinders real-world deployment. We explore the trade-off space of using alternative labeling schemes as opposed to GPT4.1 scale LLMs, with the goal of achieving near GPT level labeling quality with significantly lower cost. We develop Argo, an enterprise email labeling framework, where we construct a profiler to efficiently search the cost quality trade-off space of labeling and identify cost-efficient alternatives to labeling emails. Additionally, we design an on-demand provisioning scheme to intelligently scale Argo with real time load, to minimize cost increases during peak load inference. Over 3 open-source email datasets, Argo achieves 148-167X inference cost reduction with negligible quality degradation and 20-640000X lower profiling costs, making large-scale, context-aware email labeling practical for enterprises.

</details>


### [182] [Planning, Scheduling, and Behavior in EV Charging Systems: A Critical Survey and Trilemma Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21665)
*Peiyan Xiao,Yuheng Li,Ayan Mukhopadhyay,Sai Krishna Ghanta,Sabur Baidya,Yanhai Xiong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个三层规划-调度-行为（PSB）框架来组织电动汽车充电研究，识别了跨层集成中的保真度-可解性权衡（PSB三难困境），并指出了现有研究在处理层间耦合时的简化假设及其代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着电动汽车的快速增长，交通电气化的主要约束已从车辆采用转移到充电基础设施的部署和运营。现有研究虽然显著推进了规划、调度和行为三个层面的研究，但文献仍然碎片化，跨层交互通常通过简化假设来处理，缺乏统一的框架来组织这些研究。

Method: 开发了三层规划-调度-行为（PSB）框架，根据决策视野、行动者目标和耦合结构来组织电动汽车充电研究。该框架识别了一个保真度-可解性权衡，称为PSB三难困境：每层在孤立情况下计算困难，跨层现实集成通常需要降低至少一层的保真度。

Result: 通过回顾三个成对耦合文献（规划-调度、调度-行为、规划-行为），发现被省略的第三层通常被外生固定或用静态聚合代理表示。这些简化虽然实现了可解性，但产生了明显的代价：可能模糊长期投资反馈、时序电网和排放动态，或异质用户响应和公平性结果。

Conclusion: 基于这一诊断，识别了新兴充电技术、行为激励、公平性指标和城市规模学习方法等开放挑战，这些挑战需要在保真度、可解释性和政策相关性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: The rapid growth of electric vehicles is shifting the main constraint on transport electrification from vehicle adoption to the deployment and operation of charging infrastructure. Charging-network design requires decisions across three interdependent layers: Planning, which determines where and how much infrastructure to build; Scheduling, which governs charging dispatch, pricing, and grid interaction; and Behavior, which captures how users choose stations, charging times, and charging durations. Existing studies have advanced each layer substantially, but the literature remains fragmented, and cross-layer interactions are often treated through simplifying assumptions. This survey develops a three-layer Planning-Scheduling-Behavior (PSB) framework to organize EV charging research according to decision horizon, actor objective, and coupling structure. We further identify a fidelity-tractability tradeoff, termed the PSB trilemma: each layer is computationally difficult in isolation, and realistic integration across layers generally requires reducing the fidelity of at least one layer. Reviewing the three pairwise-coupling literatures - Planning-Scheduling, Scheduling-Behavior, and Planning-Behavior - we show that the omitted third layer is typically fixed exogenously or represented by a static aggregate surrogate. These simplifications enable tractability but impose distinct costs: they can obscure long-term investment feedback, temporal grid and emissions dynamics, or heterogeneous user response and equity outcomes. Building on this diagnosis, we identify open challenges in emerging charging technologies, behavioral incentives, equity metrics, and city-scale learning-based methods that balance fidelity, interpretability, and policy relevance.

</details>


### [183] [Emergence of agriculture in an artificial society of reinforcement learning agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22256)
*Gautier Hamon,Martí Sánchez-Fibla,Clément Moulin-Frier,Ricard Solé*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该研究通过构建强化学习人工社会模型，揭示了农业起源的四个关键机制：延迟奖励的个体规划、对欺骗者的社会脆弱性、社会学习的稳定作用以及锁定效应，证明了社会学习能有效抑制欺骗者并促进农业策略传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业起源是人类历史上重大的进化转型，也是理解复杂集体行为如何从简单交互中涌现的经典案例。研究旨在识别这一转型背后的普遍原则和机制。

Method: 构建一个嵌入动态生态环境的强化学习智能体人工社会系统，智能体在没有明确指令的情况下通过学习和环境修改的耦合动力学自发涌现农业实践。

Result: 发现农业转型受四个关键因素控制：个体规划（延迟奖励评估）、对欺骗者的社会脆弱性、社会学习的稳定作用、以及使农业一旦建立就不可逆转的锁定效应。社会学习作为"防火墙"抑制欺骗者入侵，促进成功策略传播，导致持续人口增长和驯化资源的非线性放大。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了连接个体决策、社会互动和生态反馈的普遍机制，突显了人工社会作为研究文化创新和重大进化转型涌现的实验平台的潜力。

Abstract: The origin of agriculture represents a major evolutionary transition and a paradigmatic example of how complex collective behaviors emerge from simple interactions. Here we introduce an artificial society of reinforcement learning agents embedded in a dynamic ecological environment to identify general principles underlying this transition. Within this system, agricultural practices emerge spontaneously - without explicit instruction - through the coupled dynamics of learning and environmental modification. We show that this transition is governed by four key ingredients: individual planning through the valuation of delayed rewards, social vulnerability to cheaters, stabilization via social learning, and an emergent lock-in effect that renders agriculture effectively irreversible once established. In particular, we demonstrate that social learning acts as a "firewall" that suppresses cheater invasion and enables the propagation of successful strategies, leading to sustained population growth and nonlinear amplification of domesticated resources. Together, these results reveal universal mechanisms linking individual decision-making, social interactions, and ecological feedbacks. More broadly, they highlight the potential of artificial societies as experimental platforms to study the emergence of cultural innovations and major evolutionary transitions.

</details>


### [184] [ACCoRD: Actor-Critic Conflict Resolution with Deep learning for O-RAN xApps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22306)
*Cezary Adamczyk,Adrian Kliks*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ACCoRD的基于人工神经网络（ANN）和PPO-Clip强化学习的方法，用于解决O-RAN中近实时RAN智能控制器内的控制冲突。实验表明，该方法在中高流量场景下显著降低了由冲突决策引起的负面网络事件，优于传统基于规则的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放无线接入网（O-RAN）中，控制冲突缓解（ConMit）是实现智能网络控制的关键环节。随着网络复杂度和控制决策数量的增加，不同控制决策之间可能产生冲突，进而导致网络性能下降。传统的基于规则的冲突解决方法在应对复杂动态环境时往往效率有限，亟需一种更具适应性和效率的解决方案。

Method: 本文提出了一种名为ACCoRD的方法，在近实时RAN智能控制器中部署一个冲突解决（CR）代理。该代理的核心是一个使用PPO-Clip强化学习算法训练的人工神经网络（ANN）。ANN负责分析网络状态和冲突控制决策数据，以推断最优的冲突解决动作。CR代理在每次解决冲突后从网络收集反馈以评估其效果，并在批训练阶段调整ANN的权重。评估基于仿真数据进行，并引入了一种新的冲突解决评估方法论。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的基于ANN的方法在中高流量场景下显著减少了由冲突控制决策引起的负面网络事件。与传统基于规则的方法相比，ACCoRD方法在冲突解决效率方面取得了显著提升。这证明了利用强化学习驱动的ANN处理网络控制冲突的有效性。

Conclusion: ACCoRD方法为O-RAN环境下的控制冲突缓解提供了一种高效、智能的解决方案。通过结合强化学习和人工神经网络，该方法能够动态适应网络状况并优化冲突解决策略。研究成果为未来RAN智能控制器的设计和优化提供了重要的技术参考和实践依据。

Abstract: Conflict Mitigation (ConMit) is a crucial part of intelligent network control in Open Radio Access Networks (O-RAN). In this paper, we propose a method named ACCoRD to resolve detected control conflicts in Near-Real Time RAN Intelligent Controller using a Conflict Resolution (CR) Agent with an Artificial Neural Network (ANN) trained with a reinforcement learning algorithm PPO-Clip. The implemented ANN analyzes data about the network and conflicting control decisions to infer optimal CR actions. The CR Agent gathers feedback from the network after each resolved conflict to assess its efficiency and adjust the ANN's weights during batch training. The evaluation of the proposed approach is based on simulation data. A new methodology for evaluating CR solutions is proposed. Results show that the proposed ANN-based method improves on the efficiency of rule-based approaches by significantly reducing negative network events caused by conflicting control decisions in medium and high traffic scenarios.

</details>


### [185] [Sibyl-AutoResearch: Autonomous Research Needs Self-Evolving Trial-and-Error Harnesses, Not Paper Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22343)
*Chengcheng Wang,Qinhua Xie,Wei He,Jianyuan Guo,Shiqi Wang,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Sibyl-AutoResearch框架，通过科学试错线束解决自主研究系统缺乏研究判断力的问题，实现了试验经验到后续研究行为的可审计转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自主研究系统虽然实现了可执行的科学工作流（提出想法、运行代码、检查结果、撰写论文），但无法产生研究判断力。系统存在试验经验丢失问题：弱证据变为文本、试点信号变为广泛主张、记忆保持文本形式、重复的过程失败不会改变后续行为。

Method: 引入Sibyl-AutoResearch自进化框架，核心是科学试错线程（Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses）。线束允许智能体运行有界试验、保留正负结果，并将经验教训路由到后续规划、验证、主张范围、调度、批评、写作和线束修复中。形式化两个可审计转换单元：试验到行为转换（将试验信号链接到后续研究行动）和试验到线束行为转换（将重复过程失败链接到系统更新）。在SIBYL文件支持的自主研究系统中实现该框架。

Result: 回顾性审计识别出8个高置信度转换事件，中位延迟为1次迭代，最大延迟为3次迭代。失败恢复注册表显示5类自然发生的失败（包括重复结果、过时数字、不支持的统计数据）被阻止、降级或路由到后续修复。这些痕迹证明了所提出的转换单元可以从现实的自主研究工作空间中恢复。

Conclusion: 通过可审计的转换路径，SIBYL框架展示了试验经验如何有效转化为后续研究行为和系统改进，为自主研究系统积累研究判断力提供了可行方案。框架和系统已在GitHub开源。

Abstract: Autonomous research systems increasingly make the scientific workflow executable: agents can propose ideas, run code, inspect results, and draft papers. But executable workflows do not by themselves produce research judgment. We analyze where current systems lose trial experience: weak evidence becomes prose, pilot signals become broad claims, memory remains textual, and recurring process failures do not change later behavior. We introduce Sibyl-AutoResearch, a self-evolving AutoResearch framework built around Scientific Trial-and-Error Harnesses. A harness lets agents run bounded trials, preserve positive and negative outcomes, and route lessons into later planning, validation, claim scope, scheduling, critique, writing, and harness repair. We formalize this through two auditable conversion units: trial-to-behavior conversion, which links trial signals to later research actions, and trial-to-harness-behavior conversion, which links recurring process failures to system updates. We implement the framework in SIBYL, a file-backed autonomous research system that exposes the state, roles, memory, gates, and artifact traces needed to inspect these conversion paths. A retrospective audit identifies eight high-confidence conversion events, with a median latency of one iteration and a maximum latency of three iterations. A recovered-failure registry further shows how five naturally occurring failure classes, including duplicate results, stale numbers, and unsupported statistics, were blocked, downgraded, or routed into later repair. These traces do not establish a comparative performance claim; they show that the proposed conversion units are recoverable from realistic autonomous-research workspaces. The SIBYL framework and system are available at https://github.com/Sibyl-Research-Team/AutoResearch-SibylSystem.

</details>


### [186] [A Generalized Nash Equilibrium-Seeking Scheme for Trauma Resuscitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22661)
*Promise Ekpo,Angelique Taylor,Lekan Molu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文将创伤复苏建模为具有耦合不等式约束的分布式广义纳什均衡(GNE)寻求博弈，以优化医疗工作者的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 创伤复苏是安全关键环境中的临床过程，需要设计和优化可量化的指标来准确捕捉医疗工作者(HCWs)的决策，以增强当前的复苏程序并改善患者预后。

Method: 将创伤复苏建模为具有耦合不等式约束的分布式广义纳什均衡(GNE)寻求博弈，在时变通信图上进行优化，并引入临床经验的新见解来模拟医疗工作者的行为。

Result: 该方法能够在考虑医疗工作者工作量、排班、能力和有限资源的情况下，促进获得最佳复苏结果。

Conclusion: 通过博弈论方法对创伤复苏过程进行建模和优化，可以为医疗工作者提供更好的决策支持，有潜力改善患者的治疗效果。

Abstract: Trauma resuscitation is a clinical process for treating life-threatening physiological disorders in safety-critical environments, driven by the experience of healthcare workers (HCWs). Designing and optimizing quantifiable metrics that accurately capture HCW decisions may augment current resuscitation procedures with the potential to improve patient outcomes. This motivates our socio-technical formulation of trauma resuscitation as a distributed generalized Nash equilibrium (GNE)-seeking game with coupled inequality constraints. This method is optimized over a time-varying communication graph. We introduce novel insights from clinical experience to model HCWs behavior. This work facilitates the best possible resuscitation outcome given HCWs workloads, schedules, competencies, and limited resources.

</details>


### [187] [Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems via Decentralized Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22721)
*Guangya Hao,Yunbo Long,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了DecentMem，一种去中心化的多智能体记忆框架，通过双池记忆结构和在线重加权机制，在多个基准测试中显著提升了准确率并降低了token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自演化多智能体系统几乎都采用集中式共享记忆库，这导致了通信和协调开销、隐私问题以及智能体多样性的丧失。

Method: 提出了DecentMem去中心化记忆框架，每个智能体维护自己的双池记忆：包含巩固过去轨迹的开发池和针对未见上下文生成候选方案的探索池。通过LLM-as-a-judge的阶段反馈在线重加权两个记忆池。

Result: 理论证明该设计保证解空间的全局可达性并达到O(log T)的累积遗憾，匹配随机老虎机下界。在三种MAS框架、多种模型骨干和五个基准测试中，相比最强集中式记忆基线提升准确率高达23.8%，相比无记忆基线提升52.5%，同时减少token使用达49%。

Conclusion: DecentMem成功实现了去中心化的多智能体记忆机制，既解决了集中式记忆的问题，又在理论和实践上都取得了优异表现，为自演化多智能体系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Self-evolving multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising route to LLM agents that continually improve from experience, with persistent memory at their foundation. However, existing designs almost exclusively adopt a centralized repository shared across agents, incurring communication and coordination overhead, raising privacy concerns, and collapsing agent diversity. We propose DecentMem, a decentralized memory framework in which each agent maintains its own dual-pool memory -- an exploitation pool of consolidated past trajectories and an exploration pool of LLM-generated candidates for unseen contexts. The two pools are reweighted online based on stage-wise feedback from an LLM-as-a-judge. Theoretically, we prove that this design guarantees global reachability of the solution space and achieves $O(\log T)$ cumulative regret, matching the stochastic bandit lower bound up to constants. In practice, across three MAS frameworks (AutoGen, DyLAN, AgentNet), three Qwen3 backbones (4B/8B/14B), two Gemma4 backbones (E2B/E4B) and five benchmarks spanning math, code, QA, and embodied tasks, DecentMem improves average accuracy by up to 23.8% over the strongest centralized memory baseline and by up to 52.5% over the no-memory baseline, while reducing token usage by up to 49%.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [188] [Benchmarking and Improving Monitors for Out-Of-Distribution Alignment Failure in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21602)
*Dylan Feng,Pragya Srivastava,Cassidy Laidlaw*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MOOD基准测试，用于系统评估大语言模型监控管线对分布外(OOD)对齐失败的检测能力，发现结合OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%，且比单纯扩大模型参数更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全和对齐失败常发生于分布外(OOD)情况，即开发者未预见的不寻常提示或响应模式。目前缺乏系统性评估LLM监控管线检测这类OOD对齐失败能力的基准测试。

Method: 1. 引入MOOD基准测试，包含受限训练集和七个具有多样对齐失败的测试集；2. 评估保护模型(安全分类器)的OOD泛化能力；3. 测试四种OOD检测器；4. 提出将保护模型与OOD检测器(马氏距离和困惑度检测器)结合的方法。

Result: 1. 保护模型在OOD情况下常无法泛化；2. 结合保护模型与马氏距离、困惑度OOD检测器可将召回率从39%提升至45%；3. 跨模型规模发现正向扩展趋势；4. 融入OOD检测的监控比使用20倍参数的保护模型获得更高召回率增益。

Conclusion: OOD检测应成为LLM监控的关键组成部分，本研究为该重要问题提供了研究基础。结合OOD检测器的方法比单纯增加模型参数更有效，为未来的安全监控研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Many safety and alignment failures of large language models (LLMs) occur due to out-of-distribution (OOD) situations: unusual prompt or response patterns that are unforeseen by model developers. We systematically study whether LLM monitoring pipelines can detect these OOD alignment failures by introducing a benchmark called Misalignment Out Of Distribution (MOOD). It is difficult to find failures that are truly OOD for off-the-shelf models trained on vast safety datasets. We sidestep this by including a restricted training set in MOOD that we use to train our own monitors, as well as seven test sets with diverse alignment failures that are outside the training distribution. Using MOOD, we find that guard models (safety classifiers) often fail to generalize OOD. To fix this, we propose combining guard models with OOD detectors. We test four types of OOD detectors and find that a combination of a guard model with Mahalanobis distance and perplexity-based OOD detectors can improve recall from 39% to 45%. We also establish positive scaling trends across model scales for monitors that combine a guard model and OOD detector; we find that incorporating OOD detection into monitoring achieves a higher recall gain than using a guard model with 20 times more parameters. Our work suggests that OOD detection should be a crucial component of LLM monitoring and provides a foundation for further work on this important problem.

</details>


### [189] [TO-Agents: A Multi-Agent AI Pipeline for Preference-Guided Topology Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21622)
*Isabella A. Stewart,Hongrui Chen,Faez Ahmed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TO-Agents，一个将自然语言设计意图与拓扑优化连接的多智能体AI框架。该框架通过视觉-语言推理和独立评判智能体迭代优化求解器参数，在60%的试验中生成了符合设计偏好的结构，相比无反馈的基线方法成功率提升高达6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑优化要求设计者手动将定性意图（如视觉风格、产品体验、可制造性）转化为求解器参数设置，而这些参数与设计偏好之间缺乏直接关联。设计者需要在低层次参数调整上耗费大量精力，而非专注于高层次的形式与功能设计。

Method: TO-Agents框架包含以下核心组件：（1）将自然语言问题描述转化为验证的求解器输入；（2）运行拓扑优化求解器并渲染三维拓扑结构；（3）使用多视角视觉-语言推理与独立评判智能体评估结果并修订求解器参数；（4）制造智能体对优选设计进行增材制造后处理。系统通过迭代反馈循环实现从意图到原型的端到端设计。

Result: 在悬臂梁基准测试和手机支架产品设计两个长周期任务中，系统在60%的试验中至少生成一个符合设计偏好（分层分支结构）的设计，成功率比无视觉或历史反馈的消融管线高出6倍。评判分数和人类评估表明，该管线能够识别有效参数杠杆、从不良修订中恢复并扩展设计探索空间。同时识别出过冲、选择性记忆、工具误用和参数推理错误等失效模式。

Conclusion: 智能体拓扑优化能够将设计者从低层次参数调整转向高层次的形式与功能规范，实现了从意图到原型的端到端设计流程。然而，要实现可靠的自主工程设计，仍需针对过冲、选择性记忆等失效模式开发相应的安全防护机制。

Abstract: Topology optimization can generate efficient structures, but designers often must manually translate qualitative intent, such as desired visual style, product experience, or manufacturability into solver settings that are not directly tied to those preferences. We present TO-Agents, a multi-agent AI framework that connects natural-language design intent with iterative topology optimization. The framework converts a human-provided problem description into validated solver inputs, runs a topology optimization solver, renders the resulting 3D topology, and uses multi-view vision-language reasoning with an independent judge agent to critique each result and revise solver parameters. We evaluate the framework on two long-horizon design tasks: a cantilever beam benchmark and a phone-stand product design. In both tasks, the designer specifies an aesthetic preference for hierarchically branched structures inspired by natural tree morphologies, and the system performs four revision cycles across ten independent replicates. TO-Agents produces at least one preference-aligned design in 60% of trials for each case study, corresponding to up to 6x more successful trials than an ablated pipeline without visual or historical feedback. Judge scores and human evaluations show that the pipeline can identify effective parameter levers, recover from poor revisions, and expand design exploration. A manufacturing agent further post-processes top-ranked designs for additive manufacturing, enabling end-to-end intent-to-prototype design. We also identify failure modes, including overshooting, selective memory, misplaced tools, and incorrect parameter reasoning. These results suggest that agentic topology optimization can shift designers from low-level parameter tuning toward higher-level specification of form and function, while highlighting safeguards needed for reliable autonomous engineering design.

</details>


### [190] [The Shape of Testimony: A Scalable Framework for Oral History Archive Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21623)
*Itamar Trainin,Renana Keydar,Amit Pinchevski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过计算分析方法对大屠杀口述证词进行大规模量化研究，发现USC Shoah Foundation与Yale Fortunoff档案的结构性差异部分成立，但两者存在显著重叠，挑战了"结构化vs自由形式"的简单二分法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大屠杀研究领域长期存在一个核心观点：USC Shoah Foundation的访谈倾向于结构化的、采访者引导的形式，而Yale Fortunoff Video Archive则更偏好自由形式、开放式的风格。这一区分影响了学术研究和后续档案的建设，但该假设缺乏大规模实证验证。本研究旨在批判性地检验这一基础性论断。

Method: 研究团队对两个档案库中超过1,600份证词进行了大规模计算分析。具体方法包括：1）话语分割技术；2）主题建模；3）基于大语言模型（LLM）的分析。通过主题连贯性、采访者-幸存者互动动态以及问题类型分布三个维度，量化评估证词的"结构化程度"。

Result: 研究结果部分证实了早期研究识别的结构性差异，但同时发现两个档案库之间存在显著重叠。这些重叠既体现在单个访谈内部，也体现在共同的叙事模式上。这一发现使通常应用于这些口述历史的简单"结构化vs自由形式"二分法变得复杂化。

Conclusion: 本研究不仅重新审视了大屠杀研究中的一个基础性论断，还提供了一个可扩展、可复制的比较语料库分析框架。作为概念验证，该研究为数字口述历史、叙事分析以及公民科学标注平台的设计提供了更广泛的应用前景，推动了计算方法在人文研究中的应用。

Abstract: Researchers in Holocaust studies have often distinguished between two styles of oral survivor testimony: the USC Shoah Foundation's interviews tend to follow a structured, interviewer-guided format, whereas the Yale Fortunoff Video Archive generally favors a more free-form, open-ended style. This distinction has influenced both scholarly research and the development of later archives. In this study, we critically examine that claim by conducting a large-scale computational analysis of more than 1,600 testimonies from both collections. Leveraging discourse segmentation, topic modeling, and large language model (LLM) based analysis, we quantify the "structuredness" level of testimonies through topic coherence, interviewer-survivor dynamics, and the distribution of question types. Our results generally corroborate the structural differences identified in earlier research, while also revealing significant overlaps between the collections, both within individual interviews and across common narrative patterns. This complicates the simple "structured vs. free-form" dichotomy often applied to these oral histories. Beyond revisiting a foundational claim in Holocaust studies, our work provides a scalable, replicable framework for comparative corpus analysis. As a proof of concept, it suggests broader applications for digital oral history, narrative analysis, and the design of citizen-science annotation platforms.

</details>


### [191] [MindLoom: Composing Thought Modes for Frontier-Level Reasoning Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21630)
*Haiyang Shen,Taian Guo,Xuanzhong Chen,Mugeng Liu,Weichen Bi,Wenchun Jing,Sixiong Xie,Zhuofan Shi,Yudong Han,Chongyang Pan,Siqi Zhong,Jinsheng Huang,Ming Zhang,Yun Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MindLoom框架，通过组合式“思维模式”工程合成前沿级推理数据，解决了现有方法在难度控制和多样性方面的不足，显著提升了模型在STEM和数学推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型推理数据合成方法难以系统性地生成前沿级推理数据。这些方法通常缺乏对问题难度结构性因素的洞察，导致生成数据的多样性受限且难以稳定控制难度。

Method: MindLoom框架将推理问题的难度视为原子级“知识-推理转换”（即思维模式）的累积。该方法首先将已有难题的解答分解为思维模式链，揭示问题构造逻辑；然后训练检索模型，将问题状态与兼容的思维模式匹配；接着通过迭代应用检索到的思维模式来组合新问题，并采用分布对齐采样以促进推理覆盖的多样性；最后通过基于展开的评判阶段标注生成问题的难度并提供正确的响应用于监督微调。

Result: 在涵盖五个STEM学科和四个数学推理任务的九个基准测试上，使用MindLoom生成数据微调的模型在多个模型系列和规模上均优于基础模型、蒸馏方法和外部数据基线。消融研究验证了各组件的贡献，分析表明MindLoom覆盖了广泛的推理模式并保持了有效的难度控制。

Conclusion: MindLoom为合成前沿级推理数据提供了一种有效途径，通过组合式思维模式工程实现了更好的难度控制和推理模式覆盖，为提升大语言模型的推理能力提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Although LLMs have made substantial progress in reasoning, systematically producing frontier-level reasoning data remains difficult. Existing synthesis methods often have limited visibility into the structural factors that govern problem difficulty, which can result in narrow diversity and unstable difficulty control. In this work, we view the difficulty of a reasoning problem as arising from the accumulation of atomic knowledge-reasoning transformations, which we term thought modes. Building on this perspective, we propose MindLoom, a framework for synthesizing frontier-level reasoning data through compositional thought mode engineering. Given a collection of hard problems with verified solutions, MindLoom first decomposes those solutions into thought mode chains that reveal each problem's construction logic. It then trains a retrieval model that matches problem states to compatible thought modes, providing guidance on which reasoning challenges to introduce during synthesis. New problems are composed by iteratively applying retrieved thought modes to seed questions, with distribution-aligned sampling to encourage diverse reasoning coverage. Finally, a rollout-based judging stage labels generated questions by difficulty and supplies judged-correct responses for supervised fine-tuning. We evaluate MindLoom on nine benchmarks covering five STEM disciplines and four mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple model families and sizes. Models fine-tuned on MindLoom-generated data achieves favorable performances over base models, distillation, and external-data baselines across the reported benchmarks. Ablation studies indicate the contribution of each component, and further analysis suggests that MindLoom covers a broad range of reasoning patterns while maintaining useful difficulty control. We have open-sourced our implementation at https://github.com/EachSheep/MindLoom.

</details>


### [192] [AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0: Data Model Expansions and Content Evaluation Framework for Using Agentic AI to Improve Integration between AOPs and New Approach Methodologies (NAMs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21645)
*Virginia K. Hench,J. Harry Caufield,Sierra A. T. Moxon,Jason M. O'Brien,Stephen W. Edwards*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0证据模型原型，通过数据模型扩展和结构优化，旨在支持计算生成的AOP和定量AOP，提升AOP-Wiki的FAIR性和AI就绪度，服务于监管科学和下一代风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AOP-Wiki的数据模型和应用基础设施存在限制，无法支持AOP的持续增长和演进。同时，代理式AI的出现为AOP-Wiki数据现代化提供了新机遇，需要更好的数据结构来支持AOP在监管科学和生物医学领域的应用。

Method: 开发AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0证据模型原型，这是证据模型系列中的第三个版本。通过数据模型扩展、证据结构化和质量改进，增强AOP框架与新方法学(NAMs)的整合，并提升数据的FAIR性和AI就绪度。

Result: 构建了AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0原型，具体展示了数据模型扩展方案，为实现计算生成的AOP和定量AOP(qAOPs)奠定基础，并改善了AOP框架与NAMs的整合能力。

Conclusion: AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0为AOP-Wiki的转型提供了清晰愿景，通过内部质量改进、证据结构化和增强FAIR性及AI就绪度，能够更好地服务于监管科学以及生物医学和One Health背景下AOP的新兴应用，支持下一代风险评估。

Abstract: Adverse Outcome Pathways (AOP) are logic models that causally link biological mechanisms that can be measured in a lab to adverse outcomes, relevant to chemical regulatory endpoints. AOPs contextualize new approach methodologies (NAMs), in vitro and in silico methods used as alternatives to animal testing and the sequential events in an AOP serve as multi-scale models spanning biological scales. The AOP-Wiki serves as the global repository for AOPs. While the AOP-Wiki has played a central role in AOP expansion over the past decade, constraints within the current data model and application infrastructure limit the AOP-Wiki from supporting continued AOP growth and evolution. Yet, the transformative power of agentic AI has re-invigorated AOP-Wiki data modernization efforts at a time when core AOP principles can be harnessed to inform use of AI for aggregating and structuring AOP-relevant information. Seizing upon this momentum, we present AOP-Wiki EMOD 3.0, the third in a series of evidence model prototypes, which concretely demonstrates data model expansions and our vision for how the AOP-Wiki might be transformed to better serve regulatory science and emergent use of AOPs in biomedical and One Health contexts. We aim to lay a foundation to support computationally-generated AOPs and quantitative AOPs (qAOPs) by focussing on solutions for AOP-Wiki internal quality improvement, evidence structuring to enhance AOP FAIRness and AI-readiness, and improved integration between the AOP framework and NAMs to better serve next generation risk assessment.

</details>


### [193] [Investigating Concept Alignment Using Implausible Category Members](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21683)
*Sunayana Rane,Brenden M. Lake,Thomas L. Griffiths*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让AI系统判断不合理的类别归属（如“橄榄是交通工具吗？”）来探测其对概念边界的理解，发现AI在多个基础概念上与人类存在显著且令人惊讶的差异，并证明了这些概念错位会导致影响AI安全的问题行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发具有类人概念理解能力的AI系统对于构建安全、可靠的系统至关重要。传统的探测方法（如询问合理的类别成员）容易让模型依赖训练数据中的模式回忆，而非真正理解概念。因此，本文提出一种替代策略，通过询问不合理的类别成员来刻画概念类别的边界，从而探测人类理所当然的那种概念级知识。

Method: 研究采用Rosch和Mervis的经典心理学研究框架，选取一组基础概念，要求AI系统将对象分配到正确的上级类别以及不匹配的上级类别中。通过比较AI系统与人类参与者在类别内和跨类别分配任务上的表现，分析两者在概念边界理解上的差异。研究还展示了这些概念错位如何转化为有问题的下游行为。

Result: 研究揭示了AI模型在多个概念上与人类存在有意义且令人惊讶的差异：模型将“词语”归类为“交通工具”和“服装”等类别；将多个“蔬菜”类别成员识别为“水果”；将非武器类别的样例分配到“武器”类别。这些概念错位在下游任务中导致了具有AI安全影响的问题行为。

Conclusion: AI系统在概念边界的理解上与人类存在显著差异，表现出概念错位现象。这些错位不仅揭示了模型概念理解的局限性，还会转化为影响AI安全的问题行为，对开发安全可靠的AI系统具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Developing AI systems with a human-like understanding of everyday concepts is a key step towards developing safe, reliable systems whose behavior makes sense to humans. When probing concept understanding, asking questions about plausible category members (e.g., "Is a car a vehicle?") is likely to recall patterns in the model's vast training data. We pursue an alternative strategy, characterizing the boundaries of conceptual categories by asking about implausible category members (e.g., "Is an olive a vehicle?") to probe the kind of concept-level knowledge we take for granted in fellow humans. We characterize concept boundaries for a set of fundamental concepts by studying AI systems' assignments of objects to superordinate categories from a classic psychological study by Rosch and Mervis, as well as their assignments of the same objects to mismatched superordinate categories. We compare these assignments to those made by human participants on the full range of within-category and cross-category assignment tasks. Our results reveal a range of concepts for which which models differ in meaningful and surprising ways from humans, including treating "words" as belonging to categories like "vehicles" and "clothing," identifying several "vegetable" category members as "fruit," and assigning exemplars from non-weapon categories to the "weapons" category. We also demonstrate how these instances of concept misalignment translate into problematic downstream behavior with implications for AI safety.

</details>


### [194] [The Impact of AI Usage and Informativeness on Skill Development in Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21695)
*Shang Wu,Hongyu Yao,Catarina Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI使用量与技能发展呈负相关：重度使用者表现较差，轻度使用者与不使用者相当。低信息量AI损害学习，高信息量AI提升短期表现且不损害后期表现。AI既可能补充也可能替代人类推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI在人类问题解决中的应用日益增加，但其对个人技能发展的影响尚不明确。研究旨在探究AI使用和信息量如何影响在受控逻辑推理任务中的学习效果。

Method: 采用受控实验设计，让被试在逻辑推理任务中按需获取AI辅助，分析AI使用程度和信息量对即时表现及移除AI辅助后表现的影响。

Result: 重度AI使用者表现劣于可比同伴，轻度使用者与不使用AI者表现相当；低信息量AI既不改善即时表现也不保护移除辅助后的表现，且整体学习效果较弱；高信息量AI改善短期表现且平均不损害后期表现，但存在异质性效应。

Conclusion: AI的影响具有情境依赖性：既可通过放大独立推理来补充人类技能发展，也可作为替代品削弱推理能力。因此，在存在AI辅助的环境中，调节AI访问和使用对促进技能发展至关重要。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is being increasingly integrated into human problem-solving, yet its effects on individual skill development remain unclear. We examine how both AI usage and informativeness can shape learning in the context of a controlled logical reasoning task with on-demand access to AI assistance. We find that greater AI usage is associated with weaker skill development: heavy AI users underperform relative to comparable peers, whereas light AI users perform similarly to matched users who do not use AI. We also find in our study that these patterns are mediated by AI informativeness. Low-information AI neither improves immediate performance nor preserves performance after AI assistance is removed, and is linked to weaker learning overall. On the other hand, high-information AI was found to improve short-run performance without reducing post-AI outcomes on average in our experiments, but with heterogeneous effects. Our findings in general suggest that AI can, depending on context, either complement human skill development by amplifying independent reasoning or can act as a substitute that undermines such reasoning, with the implication that regulating AI access and usage will be important for promoting skill development in the presence of AI assistance.

</details>


### [195] [Latent-space Attacks for Refusal Evasion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21706)
*Giorgio Piras,Raffaele Mura,Fabio Brau,Maura Pintor,Luca Oneto,Fabio Roli,Battista Biggio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将拒绝抑制重新定义为针对线性探测器的潜在空间规避攻击，提出了一种受控潜在空间规避方法，在15个模型上实现了最优的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通过消融拒绝方向来抑制语言模型的拒绝行为，但缺乏对其潜在空间转换原理的理论解释。本文旨在提供理论基础并改进攻击效果。

Method: 将拒绝抑制重新定义为针对线性探测器的潜在空间规避攻击；现有方法的消融操作实际上是投影到决策边界上；提出受控潜在空间规避(CLE)攻击，将表示投影到边界之外的顺从区域，并优化置信度。

Result: 在15个指令微调、多模态和推理模型上实现了最先进的攻击成功率，优于现有的拒绝消融基线和专门的越狱攻击方法。

Conclusion: 线性探测器的视角成功解释了现有方法的有效性和局限性；通过将表示进一步推向顺从区域而非仅停留在决策边界，可以显著提升攻击成功率。

Abstract: Safety-aligned language models are trained to refuse harmful requests, yet refusal behavior can be suppressed by steering their internal representations. Existing methods do so by ablating a refusal direction from model activations, aiming to remove refusal from the model's residual stream. Despite their empirical success, these methods lack a principled account of the latent-space transformation they induce and why it suppresses refusal. In this work, we recast refusal suppression as a latent-space evasion attack against linear probes trained to separate refused from answered prompts. Under this view, prior work's difference-in-means direction naturally defines such a probe, and its ablation is exactly a projection onto its decision boundary, i.e., a minimum-confidence evasion attack. This perspective not only explains the empirical success of prior work but also admits a key limitation: evasion stops at the decision boundary, motivating the need to push representations further into the compliant region, i.e., where the model answers. We leverage this by proposing a Controlled Latent-space Evasion attack that projects representations past the boundary with an optimized confidence. We achieve state-of-the-art attack success rate across 15 instruction-tuned, multimodal, and reasoning models, outperforming existing refusal-ablation baselines and specialized jailbreak attacks.

</details>


### [196] [Trace2Skill: Verifier-Guided Skill Evolution for Long-Context EDA Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21810)
*Zijian Du,Nathaniel Pinckney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Trace2Skill框架，这是一种无需RTL专用模型微调的测试时扩展方法，通过从轨迹中挖掘成功/失败模式并转化为技能，结合密集验证器反馈，显著提升了硬件LLM代理在复杂Verilog设计问题上的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂Verilog设计问题（CVDP）对硬件LLM代理构成巨大挑战，因为需要在大规模代码库中定位相关的RTL、测试平台、包含路径和构建依赖关系，进行精确编辑，并从稀疏的隐藏验证器故障中恢复。现有方法依赖昂贵的高质量微调数据或专用RTL模型训练。

Method: Trace2Skill将代理的自然语言技能视为可进化策略：1) 从重复的rollout轨迹中挖掘成功和失败模式；2) 将其转化为密集诊断和oracle教训；3) 使用oracle-mutator-selector循环生成任务特定技能来指导后续搜索、编辑、验证和恢复。此外，引入有界运行时密集验证器反馈，返回净化的功能观察结果，同时保持隐藏测试装置不可访问。

Result: 在击败种子CVDP代理的困难CVDP任务上（包括击败前沿编程代理的任务），配备密集验证器反馈的Trace2Skill显著提高了任务通过率，并在先前未解决的任务上实现了突破性通过，且无需高质量微调数据、专用RTL模型训练或模型权重更新。

Conclusion: Trace2Skill提供了一种通用的测试时扩展策略，无需模型权重更新即可显著提升硬件代理性能，该方法可扩展到数字设计之外的其他可验证EDA任务，为测试时计算扩展提供了新范式。

Abstract: Complex Verilog Design Problems (CVDP) challenge hardware LLM agents because solving them requires localizing verifier-relevant RTL, testbenches, include paths, and build dependencies inside large repository snapshots, making precise edits, and recovering from sparse hidden-verifier failures. We present Trace2Skill, a test-time scaling framework that improves a hardware agent without RTL-specialized model fine-tuning. Rather than training a new model or only sampling more candidate solutions, Trace2Skill treats the agent's natural-language skill as an evolvable policy. It mines repeated rollout traces for success and failure modes, converts them into dense diagnostics and oracle lessons, and uses an oracle, mutator, and selector loop to produce task-specific skills that guide later search, editing, validation, and recovery. Because final pass/fail labels are often too coarse for hard failures, Trace2Skill also supports bounded runtime dense verifier feedback that returns sanitized functional observations while keeping hidden harnesses and reference solutions inaccessible to the agent. This feedback helps guide skill evolution and agent execution by connecting skill text, verifier evidence, and downstream behavior. Across hard CVDP tasks that defeat the seed CVDP agent, including tasks that also defeat frontier coding agents, Trace2Skill with dense verifier feedback substantially improves task pass rates and produces breakthrough passes on previously unsolved tasks, without requiring high-quality fine-tuning data, specialized RTL model training, or model weight updates. The same framework provides a general test-time scaling strategy that can extend beyond digital design to other verifiable EDA tasks.

</details>


### [197] [AttuneBench: A Conversation-Based Benchmark for LLM Emotional Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21739)
*Kate M. Lubrano,Faisal Sayed,Ankita Rathod,Akshansh,Craver Corbyn Thomas-Smith,Mark E. Whiting,Karina Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AttuneBench基准，用于评估大语言模型在真实多轮对话中的情感智能表现，发现情感智能可分解为独立能力，偏好预测比情感识别更能区分模型能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感智能基准存在局限性：依赖合成提示、单轮对话或第三方标注，无法直接测量模型在真实对话过程中如何推断和回应用户的情感状态。随着LLM在日常生活中承担对话角色，评估其情感智能变得日益重要。

Method: 构建了AttuneBench基准，基于200场真实的人机多轮对话，参与者在对话中与匿名LLM交流，并对每一轮对话中的自身情感状态、模型行为和偏好回应进行标注。该基准评估情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和回应质量判断四个维度。

Result: 对11个模型评估发现：情绪识别、行为分类、偏好预测和回应质量判断的模型排名基本独立，表明情感智能行为可分解为分离的能力。偏好对齐和回应质量判断比情感标签准确性更能区分模型能力差异。

Conclusion: 情感智能行为需要预测特定用户在上下文中所期望的回应类型，这种能力无法通过聚合评分或单轮、合成格式直接捕捉。AttuneBench为评估这些能力提供了框架，能够诊断模型在情感显著对话中的优势和失败模式。

Abstract: Emotional intelligence (EI), the ability to perceive, understand, and respond appropriately to others' emotional states, is central to human communication, and increasingly important to assess as LLMs assume conversational roles in everyday life. Existing EI benchmarks rely on synthetic prompts, single-turn cases, or third-party annotation. These approaches do not directly measure how models infer and respond to a participant's emotional state over the course of a real conversation. We introduce AttuneBench, a benchmark grounded in 200 genuine multi-turn human-model conversations in which participants conversed with anonymized LLMs and provided turn-by-turn annotations of their emotional state, the model's behavior, and their preferred responses. Across 11 evaluated models, we find that model rankings on emotion recognition, behavioral classification, preference prediction, and judged response quality are largely independent, indicating that emotionally intelligent behavior decomposes into separable capabilities. Preference alignment and response-quality judgments are substantially more model-discriminating than emotion-label accuracy. These results indicate that emotionally intelligent behavior requires predicting what kind of response a specific user wants in context, a distinction that aggregate scoring can obscure and that single-turn or synthetic formats cannot directly capture across turns. AttuneBench provides a framework for assessing each of these capabilities and for diagnosing model-specific strengths and failure modes in emotionally salient conversation.

</details>


### [198] [AI-Enabled Serious Games: Integrating Intelligence and Adaptivity in Training Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21962)
*Priyamvada Tripathi,Bill Kapralos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了AI在严肃游戏中支持实时教学适应的潜力与挑战，区分了教学智能与适应性，并探讨了LLM、强化学习和智能体架构的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严肃游戏在医疗、国防和教育等领域广泛应用，但面临场景设计静态、创作瓶颈、学习者建模有限和实时教学适应困难等持续挑战。AI的最新进展为解决这些限制提供了新能力，但同时也引发了有效性、透明度、系统控制和学习者信任等重要问题。

Method: 本章通过历史综合方法，追溯自适应学习系统的发展，从早期计算机辅助教学到智能辅导系统(ITS)、动态难度调整(DDA)、创作平台、学习分析，直至最新的AI驱动架构。在此基础上，分析大型语言模型(LLM)、强化学习(RL)和智能体架构如何促进严肃游戏中更集成的智能性和适应性。

Result: 明确了教学智能（系统推断学习者知识并推理教学响应的能力）与适应性（在交互过程中修改教学行为的能力）的区分。识别了AI驱动系统面临的实际和研究挑战，包括可解释性、验证、计算成本，以及关于AI驱动严肃游戏长期学习效果的实证证据有限等问题。

Conclusion: AI技术为严肃游戏的实时教学适应提供了重要机遇，但要实现有效的AI集成，需要解决有效性、透明度、系统控制、学习者信任等关键问题，并建立更充分的长期学习效果实证基础。

Abstract: Serious games are widely used for learning and training across domains such as healthcare, defense, and education. Persistent challenges remain, however, including static scenario design, authoring bottlenecks, limited learner modeling, and difficulty implementing meaningful real-time instructional adaptation. Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI) introduce novel capabilities such as dynamic scenario variation, contextual feedback, adaptive pacing, and learner-state modeling that may help address some of these limitations. At the same time, integrating AI into serious games raises important questions related to validity, transparency, system control, and learner trust. This chapter examines how contemporary AI approaches may support real-time instructional adaptation in serious games. It distinguishes between instructional intelligence, defined as a system's capacity to infer learner knowledge and reason about pedagogically appropriate responses, and adaptivity, defined as the ability to modify instructional actions during interaction. A historical synthesis of adaptive learning systems is presented, tracing developments from early computer-assisted instruction through intelligent tutoring systems (ITS), dynamic difficulty adjustment (DDA), authoring platforms, learning analytics, and recent AI-enabled architectures. Building on this perspective, the chapter discusses how large language models (LLMs), reinforcement learning (RL), and agent-based architectures may contribute to more integrated forms of intelligence and adaptivity in serious games. It also highlights practical and research challenges associated with AI-enabled systems, including explainability, validation, computational cost, and the limited empirical evidence regarding long-term learning outcomes in AI-enabled serious games.

</details>


### [199] [SMDD-Bench: Can LLMs Solve Real-World Small Molecule Drug Design Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21740)
*Kevin Han,Renfei Zhang,Kathy Wei,Hamed Mahdavi,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Amir Farimani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SMDD-Bench，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）在真实世界小分子药物设计（SMDD）任务中性能的标准化基准测试。该基准包含502个可解决的任务实例，涵盖5种任务类型和102个独特的蛋白质靶标，横跨广阔的化学空间。对7个前沿LLM的测试显示，表现最好的GPT-5.4仅能解决40.2%的任务，表明当前模型在完全自主计算药物设计方面仍有巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM代理在科学发现应用中具有巨大潜力，但其在真实世界、跨多样化化学和靶标的小分子药物设计（SMDD）任务上的性能尚不清晰。现有的评估方法存在以下问题：临时性强、过于简单不适合真实世界发现、规模受限或仅限于单轮问答。因此，迫切需要一个标准化的基准测试来全面评估LLM代理在小分子设计任务中的能力。

Method: 作者构建了SMDD-Bench基准测试，这是一个具有挑战性、多轮次、长视野的代理基准。该基准包含502个保证可解的任务实例，涵盖5种任务类型：2D药效团识别、相互作用点发现、骨架跃迁、先导物优化和片段组装。这些任务跨越广阔的化学空间，涉及102个独特的蛋白质靶标。完全解决该基准需要具备强大的化学和生物学推理能力、三维直觉、理解专业工具使用以及在有限的预言机调用中展示规划能力。作者对7个前沿的开源和闭源LLM进行了基准测试。

Result: 对7个前沿LLM的基准测试结果表明，即使表现最好的模型GPT-5.4，也仅能解决40.2%的任务。这说明当前最先进的LLM在处理需要深度化学和生物学推理、3D直觉以及复杂规划能力的真实世界药物设计任务时，性能仍有显著不足，距离实现完全自主的计算药物设计目标还有很大差距。

Conclusion: SMDD-Bench为评估LLM代理在小分子药物设计领域的能力提供了一个标准化的测试平台。研究结果揭示了当前LLM在真实世界药物发现任务中的局限性，希望能够激发该领域对训练和评估完全自主计算药物设计LLM代理的研究热情。作者通过公共排行榜（smddbench.com）促进社区参与和进步。

Abstract: LLM agents have incredible potential for scientific discovery applications. However, the performance of LLM agents on real-world, small molecule drug design (SMDD) tasks across diverse chemistries and targets is unclear. Current evaluation methods are either ad hoc, too simple for real-world discovery, limited in scale, or restricted to single-turn question answering. In effort to standardize the evaluation of LLM agents on small molecule design, we introduce SMDD-Bench, a challenging, multi-turn, long-horizon agentic benchmark consisting of 502 guaranteed-solvable task instances spanning 5 task types: 2D Pharmacophore Identification, Interaction Point Discovery, Scaffold Hopping, Lead Optimization, and Fragment Assembly. SMDD-Bench tasks span a wide region of chemical space and involve 102 unique protein targets. Completely solving the benchmark would require having strong chemical and biological reasoning and 3D intuition, understanding specialized tool use, and displaying planning expertise over a limited number of oracle calls. We benchmark 7 frontier open and closed source LLMs and find even the most performant LLM, GPT5.4, solves only 40.2\% of tasks. We hope SMDD-Bench provides a standardized testbed to invigorate the field towards training and evaluating LLM agents for fully autonomous computational drug design. We host a public leaderboard at smddbench.com .

</details>


### [200] [The Log is the Agent: Event-Sourced Reactive Graphs for Auditable, Forkable Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21997)
*Yohei Nakajima*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActiveGraph提出了一种以仅追加事件日志为核心的新型智能体运行时架构，通过将日志作为事实来源、工作图作为日志的确定性投影，实现了确定性重放、低成本分叉和端到端血缘追踪三大特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体框架以语言模型为中心，将对话循环、工具、规则置于核心，日志仅作为附加的可观测性层，状态以可检索"记忆"形式持久化，缺乏确定性重放、低成本分支和完整的血缘追踪能力。

Method: 采用逆向设计思路：仅追加事件日志作为唯一事实来源；工作图是日志的确定性投影；行为（普通函数、类、LLM例程或附加在类型化边上的逻辑）响应图的变化并发出新事件；组件间无直接指令，通过共享图完全协调。提出了使重放正确的确定性契约。

Result: 该设计实现了三个检索-摘要记忆系统无法提供的特性：从日志确定性重放任意运行；在任意事件处分支运行而无需重新执行共享前缀的低成本分叉；从高层目标追溯到产生每个工件的模型调用的端到端血缘。在尽职调查示例中，完整因果结构可仅从日志重建。

Conclusion: ActiveGraph为自改进智能体提供了理想基础，扩展了BabyAGI谱系和先前的图记忆研究。该架构通过事件溯源模式实现了传统记忆系统无法提供的关键特性，为智能体系统的可调试性、可追溯性和演化能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: Most agent frameworks are built around the language model: a conversation loop comes first, then tools, then rules, and finally a logging layer bolted on for observability, with state persisted as retrievable "memory." We describe ActiveGraph, a runtime that inverts this arrangement. The append-only event log is the source of truth; the working graph is a deterministic projection of that log; and behaviors--ordinary functions, classes, LLM-backed routines, or logic attached to typed edges--react to changes in the graph and emit new events. No component instructs another; coordination happens entirely through the shared graph. This single design decision yields three properties that retrieval-and-summarization memory systems do not provide: deterministic replay of any run from its log, cheap forking that branches a run at any event without re-executing the shared prefix, and end-to-end lineage from a high-level goal down to the individual model call that produced each artifact. We present the architecture, a determinism contract that makes replay sound, and a worked diligence example whose full causal structure is reconstructable from the log alone. We discuss--without claiming to demonstrate--why this substrate is unusually well suited to self-improving agents, and how it extends the BabyAGI lineage and prior graph-memory research.

</details>


### [201] [Who Uses AI? Platforms, Workforce, and AI Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21743)
*Michelle Yin,Burhan Ogut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI平台对话日志测量的职业暴露度存在显著测量误差，主要反映平台用户特征而非整体劳动力市场，导致就业效应估计严重偏误，需要重新加权修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛使用AI平台对话日志来衡量职业对AI的暴露程度，但这种测量方法可能存在根本性问题——它测量的可能是平台用户群体特征而非真实劳动力市场特征，导致后续就业效应分析产生偏误。

Method: 作者通过控制实验设计，固定结果变量、样本、控制变量和估计量，仅改变平台输入数据，比较不同AI平台和同一供应商不同渠道（消费者vs企业）的估计差异；采用BLS劳动力市场份额重新加权进行修正；理论上推导非经典测量误差的概率极限和偏识别边界。

Result: 不同平台输入数据导致ChatGPT后就业系数变化达1.9倍；同一供应商的消费者与企业渠道估计结果符号相反；重新加权后估计值缩减42-93%；测量误差导致就业弹性估计有偏，且低估替代效应的程度大于低估增强效应。

Conclusion: 基于AI平台对话日志的职业暴露度测量存在系统性偏误，现有研究的估计结果需要谨慎解读，重新加权方法可有效缓解偏差，理论研究揭示了测量误差的非经典性质及其对政策含义的影响。

Abstract: A growing literature uses artificial intelligence platform conversation logs to measure occupation exposure. We show that these scores partly measure platform user base rather than the workforce. Holding outcome, sample, controls, and estimator fixed while varying only the platform input changes the post-ChatGPT employment coefficient by a factor of 1.9, and within-vendor consumer-versus-enterprise channels produce estimates that disagree in sign. Reweighting to Bureau of Labor Statistics workforce shares attenuates estimates by 42 to 93 percent. We formalize the non-classical measurement error, derive probability limits and partial-identification bounds for employment elasticities. The bias understates substitution more than augmentation.

</details>


### [202] [Cross-domain benchmarks reveal when coordinated AI agents improve scientific inference from partial evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22300)
*Fiona Y. Wong,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究构建了一个跨领域基准测试，用于评估协调式AI代理相对于简单科学工作流程的价值。实验涵盖四个科学任务，定义了三种运行机制：跨通道组合在多学科部分捕获现象时表现优异（AUROC超过0.94），但当单一信号主导时，协调主要改善可解释性和可追溯性，而非预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学证据通常跨越仪器、数据库和学科，没有单一来源能够记录完整现象。这使得确定协调式AI代理相比更简单的科学工作流程何时能增加价值变得困难。研究旨在系统评估这一问题。

Method: 构建跨领域基准测试，涵盖四个科学任务：分子结构音乐映射、科学范式转移检测、媒介传播疾病出现识别、以及凌日系外行星候选验证。每个案例采用冻结评估面板、预定义评分协议、显式基线、消融或零对照，以及明确限制。使用ScienceClaw x Infinite提供可审计工件和溯源层。

Result: 定义了三种运行机制：（1）跨通道组合在多学科部分捕获现象时超越单通道基线：气候-病媒出现检测达AUROC 0.944，系外行星验证达AUROC 0.955；（2）系外行星工作流与强组合摘要基线基本持平，表明分解不一定改善顶尖性能；（3）范式转移检测中协调主要改善解释性和可追溯性；分子声化主要提供表征而非预测增益。

Conclusion: 基准测试仅在相应的性能、溯源或表征主张得到显式比较器支持时，才为协调赋予价值。这为评估协调式AI系统在科学研究中的实际价值提供了系统框架。

Abstract: Scientific evidence often spans instruments, databases, and disciplines, so no single source records the full phenomenon. This makes it difficult to determine when coordinated AI agents add value over simpler scientific workflows. We evaluate this question with a cross-domain benchmark spanning four scientific tasks: mapping molecular structure into musical representations, detecting historical paradigm shifts in science, identifying vector-borne disease emergence, and vetting transiting-exoplanet candidates. Each case uses a frozen evaluation panel, predefined scoring protocols, explicit baselines, ablations or null controls, and stated limitations. The results define three operating regimes. When different disciplines each capture only part of the phenomenon, cross-channel composites improve over single-channel baselines: climate-vector emergence reaches AUROC 0.944 and exoplanet vetting reaches AUROC 0.955. However, the exoplanet workflow is effectively tied with a strong combined-summary baseline, showing that decomposition does not always improve top-line performance. When one signal dominates, as in paradigm-shift detection, coordination mainly improves interpretation and traceability. For molecular sonification, the gain is representational rather than predictive. ScienceClaw x Infinite provides the auditable artifact and provenance layer for this evaluation. The benchmark therefore assigns value to coordination only when the corresponding performance, provenance, or representation claim is supported by explicit comparators.

</details>


### [203] [A Causal Argumentation Method for Explainability of Machine Learning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21758)
*Henry Salgado,Meagan R. Kendall,Martine Ceberio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将因果发现与论证推理相结合的新型可解释AI方法，通过构建双极论证框架来解释模型预测的深层原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释AI方法通常只能识别与模型预测相关的特征，但无法清晰解释模型为何做出特定决策，缺乏对决策逻辑的深层理解。

Method: 首先利用因果发现方法识别变量间的因果关系，然后将其转化为双极论证框架（BAF）表示特征间的支持与对立交互，最后采用半稳定语义寻找能够解释特定结果的特征扩展集。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性，并与标准事后可解释性方法进行了对比分析，证明了该方法能够提供更深入的预测解释。

Conclusion: 通过整合因果性与论证推理，该方法成功填补了传统可解释AI方法在解释"为何"做出决策方面的空白，为提升AI系统的可解释性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Explainable AI (XAI) methods identify which features are relevant to a model's predictions but often fail to clarify why certain decisions are made. In this work, we present a novel method that integrates causality with argument-based reasoning to explain why models may be making predictions. Our approach first identifies causal relationships among variables using causal discovery methods and then translates these into a Bipolar Argumentation Framework (BAF) to represent supportive and opposing interactions among features. By using semi-stable semantics, we find extensions of features that explain why certain outcomes may have been chosen. We demonstrate our method on two benchmark datasets and compare its results against standard post-hoc explainability approaches.

</details>


### [204] [LCGuard: Latent Communication Guard for Safe KV Sharing in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.22786)
*Sadia Asif,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri,Momin Abbas,Prasanna Sattigeri,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCGuard框架，用于保护多智能体LLM系统中基于KV缓存的潜在通信安全，防止敏感信息通过共享缓存泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在通信过程中，KV缓存编码了上下文输入、中间推理状态和智能体特定信息，可能在不经显式文本披露的情况下，通过不透明通道将敏感内容传播到其他智能体，存在隐私泄露风险。

Method: 提出LCGuard框架，将共享KV缓存视为潜在工作记忆，在缓存构件跨智能体传输前学习表征级变换。通过重构方法形式化定义表征级敏感信息泄露：若对抗解码器能从共享缓存构件中恢复智能体特定敏感输入，则该构件不安全。采用对抗训练方法，对手学习重构敏感输入，而LCGuard学习保留任务相关语义并减少可重构信息的变换。

Result: 在多个模型族和多智能体基准测试上的实证评估表明，与标准KV共享基线相比，LCGuard能持续降低基于重构的泄露和攻击成功率，同时保持具有竞争力的任务性能。

Conclusion: LCGuard能有效防御多智能体LLM系统中KV缓存潜在通信的敏感信息泄露问题，在保障隐私安全的同时不显著牺牲任务性能，为安全的多智能体协作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on intermediate communication to coordinate complex tasks. While most existing systems communicate through natural language, recent work shows that latent communication, particularly through transformer key-value (KV) caches, can improve efficiency and preserve richer task-relevant information. However, KV caches also encode contextual inputs, intermediate reasoning states, and agent-specific information, creating an opaque channel through which sensitive content may propagate across agents without explicit textual disclosure. To address this, we introduce \textbf{LCGuard} (Latent Communication Guard), a framework for safe KV-based latent communication in multi-agent LLM systems. LCGuard treats shared KV caches as latent working memory and learns representation-level transformations before cache artifacts are transmitted across agents. We formalize representation-level sensitive information leakage operationally through reconstruction: a shared cache artifact is unsafe if an adversarial decoder can recover agent-specific sensitive inputs from it. This leads to an adversarial training formulation in which the adversary learns to reconstruct sensitive inputs, while LCGuard learns transformations that preserve task-relevant semantics and reduce reconstructable information. Empirical evaluations across multiple model families and multi-agent benchmarks show that LCGuard consistently reduces reconstruction-based leakage and attack success rates while maintaining competitive task performance compared to standard KV-sharing baselines.

</details>


### [205] [What Counts as AI Sycophancy? A Taxonomy and Expert Survey of a Fragmented Construct](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.21778)
*Meryl Ye,Lujain Ibrahim,Jessica Y. Bo,Myra Cheng,Ida Mattsson,Daniel Vennemeyer,Robert Kraut,Steve Rathje*

Main category: cs.AI
